Motivation: 现有VLA模型难以利用触觉反馈实现轻柔操作，主要因为缺乏对齐的视觉-触觉-语言数据以及有效的闭环力反馈机制。

Method: 提出Tabero基准，包含数据高效管线（利用开源操作轨迹生成视觉-触觉-语言任务）和多维评估协议；提出Tabero-VTLA架构，采用解耦力-位置命令接口和固定混合控制器实现实时力感知操作。

Result: 在保持高任务成功率的同时，在轻柔指令下平均夹持力降低超过70%。

Conclusion: Tabero展示了基于多模态经验调节交互力的能力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for robots to achieve human-like gentle manipulation. However, existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models struggle to exploit tactile feedback for gentle manipulation due to scarce aligned vision-tactile-language data and the lack of effective closed-loop force feedback mechanisms. To address these challenges, we introduce Tabero, a benchmark and model suite for gentle, language-conditioned robotic manipulation that demands fine-grained contact force perception. First, the Tabero benchmark addresses the scarcity of tactile data by presenting a data-efficient pipeline that repurposes open-source robot manipulation trajectories to generate diverse vision-tactile-language tasks, and establishes a multidimensional evaluation protocol that measures task success alongside physical interaction quality. Second, we propose Tabero-VTLA, an architecture with a decoupled force-position command interface; the resulting force-position commands are executed by a fixed hybrid controller to enable real-time, force-aware manipulation. Evaluated on Tabero, our model maintains high task success while reducing average grip force by over 70\% under gentle instructions, demonstrating its ability to modulate interaction forces based on multimodal experience. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/NathanWu7/Tabero.

</details>


### [59] [S-Cheetah: A Novel Quadrupedal Robot with a 3-DOF Active Spine Learning Agile Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27909)
*Zimu Li,Weibang Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Cheetah，一种配备3自由度仿生主动脊柱的四足机器人，通过加速度课程学习和定制奖励函数的强化学习框架，实现了6.9 m/s的疾驰速度和7.2 rad/s的原地转向率，并展示了猫科动物式空中自翻正能力，证明三轴旋转脊柱全面增强了四足机器人的运动敏捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人脊柱研究多简化自由度以降低控制复杂度，无法实现生物脊柱的空间三轴旋转特性，因此复制多自由度仿生脊柱并有效利用其增强四足机器人敏捷运动仍是一个重要研究挑战。

Method: 设计了具有3-DOF仿生串联主动脊柱的四足机器人S-Cheetah；开发了一个专门的强化学习框架，通过加速度课程学习策略以及疾驰步态奖励、脊柱波动奖励、脊柱转向奖励等定制奖励函数，主动促进脊柱参与并最大化机器人运动能力。

Result: S-Cheetah达到6.9 m/s的峰值速度（旋转G2疾驰步态），7.2 rad/s的原地转向率；展现出猫科动物启发的空中自翻正能力，能从任意方向自由落体稳定四足落地；通过多种运动任务验证了3-DOF脊柱全面增强了运动敏捷性。

Conclusion: 提出的3-DOF主动脊柱结合强化学习框架，可全面增强四足机器人的运动敏捷性，包括高速疾驰、快速转向和空中自翻正等能力。

Abstract: The biological spine of quadrupeds enables sagittal flexion/extension, lateral bending, and axial rotation, playing a crucial role in highly agile and dexterous locomotion. While numerous studies have integrated active spinal joints into quadrupedal robots to enhance agility, most designs simplify control complexity by reducing spinal degrees of freedom (DOF), failing to achieve the spatial tri-axial rotation characteristic of biological spines. Consequently, replicating a multi-DOF biomimetic spine and effectively leveraging it to empower the agile locomotion of quadrupedal robots remains a significant research challenge. In this study, we present S-Cheetah, a quadrupedal robot featuring a 3-DOF bio-inspired serial active spine capable of biomimetic spatial tri-axial rotation. To empower the robot to fully utilize this active spine, we developed a specialized reinforcement learning framework to actively promote the engagement of the introduced spine and maximize the robot's locomotive capabilities by integrating an acceleration curriculum learning strategy with tailored reward functions, such as a gallop gait reward, a spine undulation reward, and a spine steering reward. Experimental results demonstrate that S-Cheetah can achieve a peak speed of 6.9 m/s using the rotary G2 gallop gait and an in-place turning rate of 7.2 rad/s. Besides, the system exhibits an emergent, feline-inspired aerial self-righting capability, allowing it to land stably on four feet from arbitrary orientations during free fall. Finally, through extensive evaluations across diverse locomotion tasks, we prove that the introduction of the proposed 3-DOF spine comprehensively enhances the locomotive agility of quadrupedal robots. Project website: himmy-robotics.github.io/scheetah

</details>


### [60] [Frequency-Guided Action Diffusion via Sub-Frequency Manifold Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27919)
*Junlin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频率引导算子(FGO)，通过渐进式子频率流形引导改善扩散策略生成动作的平滑性，在15个机器人操作任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示教数据含高频噪声（间歇性抖动、暂停、动作震颤），扩散策略的迭代去噪会放大这些伪影，导致模型继承次优行为。

Method: 提出频率引导算子(FGO)，在扩散策略生成过程中通过渐进式扩张频谱带的中间子频率流形引导噪声样本，实现隐式频谱操控与平滑动作生成。

Result: 在5个基准的15个机器人操作任务上，FGO在提升动作平滑性和时间一致性的同时保留了任务执行所需的细节。

Conclusion: FGO通过频域引导有效解决了扩散策略中高频噪声放大问题，实现了更优的动作质量。

Abstract: Learning visuomotor policies via behavior cloning typically involves mimicking expert demonstrations collected by human operators. However, natural human demonstrations inherently contain high-frequency noise, such as intermittent jerks, pauses, and action jitter. Training policies to directly imitate these raw trajectories inevitably causes the model to inherit these suboptimal behaviors. This pathology is particularly pronounced in diffusion-based policies, where iterative denoising steps can inadvertently amplify high-frequency artifacts at the expense of meaningful fine-grained details. To address these limitations, we present a novel frequency-based algorithm that enables implicit spectral maneuvering and smooth action generation. Our method, Frequency Guidance Operator (FGO), steers the generation process of diffusion polices by progressively driving the noisy samples through intermediate sub-frequency manifolds with expanding spectral bands. Validated on 15 robotic manipulation tasks from 5 benchmarks, FGO achieves superior performance in enhancing action smoothness and temporal consistency while preserving the details necessary for successful task execution. Project website: https://henrywjl.github.io/frequency-guidance-operator/

</details>


### [61] [SANTS: A State-Adaptive Scheduler for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27947)
*Yirui Sun,Guangyu Zhuge,Keliang Liu,Jie Gu,Xinyu Bing,Zhongxue Gan,Chunxu Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SANTS，一种自适应噪声轨迹调度器，用于视频到动作的扩散策略。SANTS根据状态自适应地选择去噪深度，在保持控制性能的同时大幅减少推理冗余，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到94.4%的成功率，并将延迟降低81.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间的世界动作模型（WAMs）中，完全去噪的视频并不一定是最优的动作条件；视频去噪的深度存在一个状态依赖的最优点，超过该点后继续去噪可能反而降低动作质量。现有方法使用固定终端去噪深度，无法适应不同状态下的最优需求。

Method: 提出状态自适应噪声轨迹调度器（SANTS），在视频决策点读取当前视频状态表示和噪声水平，联合预测累积停止风险函数和相对噪声进度比。使用路径级奖励进行后训练，优化下游动作质量而非中间视频保真度，并显式惩罚冗余的视频状态更新。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上成功率达到94.4%，在7个真实机器人任务上平均成功率为73.1%，相对于完全去噪，延迟分别降低81.7%和79.0%。

Conclusion: 自适应地沿视频噪声轨迹选择去噪深度，能够在保持WAM风格未来推理的控制收益的同时，大幅消除冗余推理开销。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot manipulation by using video-based future representations to condition action generation. In pixel-space WAMs, however, the best action condition is not necessarily the fully denoised video. Controlled denoising-depth scans show that video refinement can reduce action error up to a state-dependent point, after which the gain may saturate or even reverse when late predictions become less action-relevant or physically unreliable. This suggests that action generation should use a state-dependent point along the video noise trajectory rather than a fixed terminal denoising depth. We introduce State-Adaptive Noise Trajectory Scheduler (SANTS), a lightweight scheduler for video-to-action diffusion policies. At each video decision point, SANTS reads the current video-state representation and noise level, then jointly predicts a cumulative stopping hazard and a relative noise-progression ratio. SANTS is post-trained with a path-level reward computed after the frozen action branch generates the final action chunk, so the scheduler is optimized for downstream action quality rather than intermediate video fidelity, while redundant video-state updates are explicitly penalized. Experiments show that SANTS reaches \(94.4\%\) overall success on RoboTwin 2.0 and \(73.1\%\) average success across seven real-robot tasks, while reducing latency by \(81.7\%\) and \(79.0\%\) relative to full video denoising, respectively. These results indicate that adaptive selection along the video noise trajectory can preserve the control benefits of WAM-style future reasoning while removing much of its redundant inference cost.

</details>


### [62] [VLM-Based Advanced Rider Assistance System for Motorcycle Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27948)
*Mohamed Elnoor,Francesca Baldini,Ananya Trivedi,Faizan M. Tariq,Jovin D'sa,David Isele,Sangjae Bae,Dinesh Manocha,Yosuke Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对摩托车安全性不足及ARAS系统发展滞后的问题，本文提出了一种结合视觉语言模型(VLM)进行上下文危险推理、分割检测构建稠密风险地图，并利用采样规划器结合摩托车动力学进行节气门和转向控制的先进骑手辅助系统(ARAS)。在CARLA模拟器中的实验表明，该方法相比基线实现了更高的成功率和更低的危险暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩托车相比汽车面临更高的碰撞风险，但先进骑手辅助系统(ARAS)的发展远落后于汽车的高级驾驶辅助系统(ADAS)，亟需开发针对摩托车特性的智能安全辅助技术。

Method: 1) 利用视觉语言模型(VLM)进行上下文危险推理；2) 结合分割检测构建稠密风险地图，编码语义特征(如坑洼严重度、水滑性)和物理属性(如尺寸、深度)，生成每像素危险代价；3) 采用针对摩托车动力学的采样规划器，推荐节气门和转向动作，以最小化危险暴露并朝目的地前进。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器的多种场景中，相比基线方法，该方法实现了更高的任务成功率和更低的危险暴露，定性结果展示了可解释的风险地图和安全的轨迹推荐。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于语义感知和风险感知规划的ARAS系统能够有效提升摩托车骑行安全性，其生成的可解释风险地图有助于理解系统决策，为摩托车智能安全辅助系统的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Motorcycles face disproportionately high crash risks compared to cars due to limited protection and heightened sensitivity to surface hazards, yet Advanced Rider Assistance Systems (ARAS) remain underdeveloped relative to Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS). We propose a novel ARAS that enhances motorcycle safety through semantic perception and risk-aware planning. Our approach leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for contextual hazard reasoning and integrates them with segmentation-based detection to construct dense risk maps. These maps encode both semantic characteristics (e.g., pothole severity, puddle slipperiness) and physical attributes (e.g., size, depth), which produce per-pixel hazard costs that capture motorcycle-specific risks. These maps are used by a sampling-based planner tailored to motorcycle dynamics to recommend throttle and steering actions that minimize hazard exposure while advancing toward the destination. We evaluate our system in different scenarios in the CARLA simulator. Compared to the baseline method, our method achieves higher success rates and lower hazard exposure, while qualitative results demonstrate interpretable risk maps and safe trajectory recommendations.

</details>


### [63] [How Should We Teach Robots? A Comparison of Kinesthetic, Joystick, and Gesture-Based Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28033)
*Petr Vanc,Jan Kristof Behrens,Václav Hlaváč,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过用户研究比较了三种机器人示教方式（动觉引导、摇杆遥操作、手势控制）在三个操作任务中的表现，发现动觉引导在多数任务中表现最佳，手势控制虽整体可靠性较低但在某些情况下与动觉引导效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同示教方式（动觉引导、摇杆遥操作、手势控制）在操作任务中的可用性和性能权衡，为机器人从 demonstrations 中学习提供选择依据。

Method: 在8名参与者中开展用户研究，对比三种示教方式在三个操作任务上的表现，评估指标包括重演成功率、改进的NASA-TLX工作负荷和常见示教错误。

Result: 动觉引导在多数面向姿态敏感和接触密集的任务中展现出最短示教时间、最低工作负荷和最高成功率；摇杆遥操作在简单插销拾取任务中表现最佳；手势控制整体可靠性较低但在某些情况下与动觉引导效果相当。

Conclusion: 不同示教方式各有优劣：动觉引导适用于复杂接触任务，摇杆适合简单任务，手势控制虽不可靠但有一定潜力，选择示教方式需根据任务特性权衡。

Abstract: Instructing robots from demonstrations can be done through different teaching modalities, each with different usability and performance trade-offs. This paper compares kinesthetic guidance, joystick teleoperation, and hand gestures in a user study with eight participants. We evaluate replay success, modified NASA-TLX workload, and common teaching errors across three manipulation tasks. Kinesthetic guidance produced the shortest demonstrations, lowest workload, and highest success on the more orientation-sensitive and contact-rich tasks. Joystick teleoperation performed best on simple peg picking. Hand-gesture teaching, although less reliable overall, performed better than expected and in some cases achieved results comparable to kinesthetic guidance.

</details>


### [64] [SAFEVPR: Patch-Based Conformal Verification for Safe Cross-Condition Sequence Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28048)
*Ha Sier,Jiaqiang Zhang,Zhuo Zou,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFEVPR提出了一种无需训练、基于共形预测的验证-校准流程，用于跨条件序列视觉地点识别中的安全接受/拒绝决策。该方法用MNN补丁匹配分数替换余弦相似度，并用Mondrian共形LTT进行分箱校准，在23个跨条件场景下，以目标FDR=0.10实现了23/23的经验有效性，平均接受FDR为0.014，平均TPR为0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列VPR中检索到的top-1候选是否安全接受是一个关键决策问题。共形预测提供自然框架，但其有限样本保证依赖于校准数据与测试数据之间的可交换性，而跨条件部署（如季节、光照变化）会破坏该假设，导致标准共形方法失效。

Method: 1) 用冻结DINOv2 ViT特征的互近邻(MNN)补丁匹配分数替代标准余弦相似度；2) 用Mondrian共形Learn-Then-Test(LTT)替代平坦LTT校准，在分数分箱上分别拟合经Bonferroni校正的阈值。

Result: 在Oxford RobotCar、NCLT、St Lucia数据集的23种跨条件设置上，使用三种冻结VPR主干，SAFEVPR在目标FDR=0.10下实现23/23经验有效，平均接受FDR为0.014，平均TPR为0.75。对比方法AUROC相当但失败更多，在纹理缺乏场景能安全弃权。

Conclusion: 仅靠原始判别能力不足以实现共形有效性；所提非训练式验证-校准流程能在违反可交换性的跨条件下保持经验FDR控制，提升VPR系统的安全可靠性。

Abstract: Sequence-based visual place recognition (VPR) for SLAM and robot relocalization must decide whether the retrieved top-1 candidate is safe to accept. Conformal prediction is a natural framework for this accept/reject decision, but its finite-sample guarantees rely on exchangeability between calibration and deployment (test) data, which is violated under cross-condition deployment. We introduce SAFEVPR, a non-trainable verification-and-calibration pipeline for safe cross-condition sequence VPR. SAFEVPR replaces the standard backbone cosine similarity with a mutual-nearest-neighbour (MNN) patch-matching score computed from frozen DINOv2 ViT features, and replaces flat Learn-Then-Test calibration with Mondrian conformal LTT, fitting separate Bonferroni-corrected thresholds across score bins. Under exchangeability, these thresholds would provide finite-sample false-discovery-rate (FDR) control; under condition shift, we evaluate empirical validity per deployment. Across 23 cross-condition setups from Oxford RobotCar, NCLT, and St Lucia datasets, using three frozen VPR backbones, SAFEVPR is empirically valid on 23/23 setups at target FDR alpha = 0.10, achieving mean accepted FDR 0.014 and mean true-positive rate (TPR) 0.75. The results show that raw discrimination alone is not sufficient for conformal validity: AnyLoc-VLAD and Super-Point+LightGlue reach comparable area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) but fail more setups under the same calibration. On textureless repetitive scenery, SAFEVPR safely abstains rather than accepting unreliable matches. Code is available at https://github.com/Hasar12139/SafeVPR.

</details>


### [65] [Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08758)
*Jiaxin Liu,Peng Yang,Yuping Li,Xinyue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OLSF-TRS，一种结合组合优化与多智能体强化学习的通用可扩展序列决策框架，用于处理料箱搬运机器人系统的订单履约问题。在小规模系统中接近最优（最优性差距<3.5%），在大规模系统中相比启发式方法减少8-12%料箱移动，相比SOTA规则方法减少超30%，同时保持实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电商和小批量生产发展，物流单元从托盘向料箱转变，料箱搬运机器人系统成为自动化履约中心的核心。现有研究针对特定系统的决策机制难以泛化，缺乏统一的、可扩展的订单-料箱-机器人序列决策框架。

Method: 提出Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework (OLSF-TRS)，结合结构化组合优化与多智能体强化学习，协同协调订单分配、料箱分配和机器人调度三个层面的决策。

Result: 小规模场景下平均最优性差距低于3.5%；大规模场景中，相比启发式基线减少8-12%料箱移动，相比SOTA规则方法减少超30%，且保持实时响应能力。具体包括成本降低、能耗下降和吞吐量稳定性提升。

Conclusion: OLSF-TRS为广泛部署的料箱搬运机器人系统提供了一个高效、统一的订单履约决策框架，可支持电商和工业物流领域的高质量订单履约。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid expansion of e-commerce and small-batch production, the size of the intralogistics load unit of finished goods, semi-finished goods and raw materials is steadily shrinking. Totes are gradually replacing pallets as the primary handling and storage container. This shift has propelled tote-handling robotic systems to the forefront of automation order fulfillment centers. The order-fulfillment decisions of tote-handling robotic systems share a common order-tote-robot sequential decision-making nature. Existing studies primarily focus on decision mechanisms tailored to particular systems, making it difficult to generalize or transfer them to other contexts. We propose an Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems (OLSF-TRS), a generalized and scalable sequential decision framework that combines structured combinatorial optimization with multi-agent reinforcement learning to coordinate order,tote, and robot decisions. On small-scale tote-handling robotic systems, OLSF-TRS achieves near-optimal performance with average optimality gaps below 3.5% across two distinct system configurations. In large-scale scenarios, OLSF-TRS consistently outperforms heuristic baselines across two different system types, reducing total tote movements by 8-12% and over 30% compared to SOTA rule-based approaches, while maintaining real-time responsiveness. These improvements translate into tangible operational benefits, including cost reduction, lower energy consumption, and enhanced throughput stability. The proposed framework delivers an efficient and unified order fulfillment decision-making framework for widely deployed tote-handling robotic systems,supporting high-quality order fulfillment in both e-commerce and industrial logistics sectors.

</details>


### [66] [Whose Is This?: Context-Aware Object Ownership Inference with Uncertainty-Guided Questioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28087)
*Saki Hashimoto,Akira Taniguchi,Shoichi Hasegawa,Yoshinobu Hagiwara,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出COIN框架，结合大语言模型进行上下文感知的物体所有权推断，并利用共形预测处理不确定性，在模拟家庭环境中取得了0.988的子集准确率和0.991的平均Jaccard指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需要推断物体所有权以正确理解指令（如"把我的杯子拿来"），但所有权是隐式属性无法直接观测，现有方法依赖有限线索（如近期使用记录），在临时共享等场景下不可靠。

Method: 提出COIN框架：1) 利用大语言模型融合用户背景信息和物体使用历史来估计所有权分数；2) 应用共形预测构造合理所有者集合；3) 在预测不确定时选择性生成用户查询。

Result: 在模拟家庭环境中，COIN的子集准确率达到0.988，平均Jaccard指数为0.991，在临时使用和共享所有权场景下均保持高性能，持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合上下文推理与不确定性感知交互能同时提升所有权推断的估计准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Service robots must infer object ownership to correctly interpret instructions such as "bring me my cup." However, ownership is a latent attribute that cannot be directly observed, and existing methods often rely on limited cues such as recent usage, making them unreliable in scenarios such as temporary sharing. We propose a framework for context-aware ownership inference with uncertainty-guided interaction (COIN). The method integrates user background information and object usage history using a large language model (LLM) to estimate ownership scores. To handle uncertainty, we apply conformal prediction to construct a set of plausible owners and selectively generate user queries when the prediction is uncertain. Experiments in a simulated home environment show that the proposed method consistently outperforms baseline approaches, achieving a Subset Accuracy of 0.988 and a Mean Jaccard index of 0.991. The method also maintains high performance in scenarios involving temporary use and shared ownership. The results demonstrate that combining contextual reasoning with uncertainty-aware interaction improves both estimation accuracy and robustness. The project page is available at https://emergentsystemlabstudent.github.io/COIN/.

</details>


### [67] [Integrated Exploration-Aware UAV Route Optimization and Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28654)
*Jimin Choi,Grant Stagg,Cameron K. Peterson,Max Z. Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向不确定和时变先验信息的无人机探索感知路径规划框架，将环境建模为空间风险图，将报告危险建模为不确定的兴趣区域（ROI），通过车辆路径问题优化访问ROI的路线，并利用辅助伪节点和B样条轨迹进行局部探索与信念更新。在线重规划相比离线优化平均KL散度降低15.9%，比直线遍历降低48.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在灾害监测中面临飞行续航有限、先验信息不完整且空间不精确、执行过程中信息会变化等挑战，现有方法未充分处理将报告危险建模为不确定的感兴趣区域，并有效分配续航在访问已知位置与探索未知区域之间的权衡。

Method: 1. 环境建模为空间风险图，每个位置关联危险条件的信念；2. 将报告危险建模为不确定的兴趣区域（ROI）；3. 求解车辆路径问题优化访问ROI的路线，并用辅助伪节点增强空间覆盖；4. 将剩余飞行距离预算分配至各路径段；5. 优化动态可行的B样条轨迹进行局部探索；6. 执行中通过测量更新栅格信念图，并根据新信息和剩余预算进行在线重规划。

Result: 在48种场景配置下，在线重规划相比离线优化平均KL散度降低15.9%，相比直线遍历降低48.6%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效处理不确定和时变的先验信息，在线重规划显著优于离线优化和简单直线遍历策略，验证了在复杂危险环境中探索感知权衡方法的有效性。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for exploration-driven monitoring in hazardous environments such as disaster zones, contaminated sites, wildfire areas, and damaged infrastructure, where limited flight endurance must be allocated between visiting reported locations and gathering new information. In these settings, prior information regarding hazards is often incomplete, spatially imprecise, and subject to change during execution. For example, initial reports may identify a region where a hazard is likely to exist, but the actual hazard may be displaced, partially observed, or entirely unreported. We present an integrated exploration-aware UAV route optimization and path planning framework for hazard monitoring under uncertain and evolving prior information. The environment is represented as a spatial risk map, where each location has an associated belief of hazardous conditions. Reported hazards are modeled as uncertain regions of interest (ROIs) rather than confirmed target locations, requiring the UAV to inspect reported areas while also using its limited flight endurance to explore informative regions. The proposed method solves a vehicle routing problem over reported ROIs, augments the route with auxiliary pseudo-nodes to improve spatial coverage, allocates the remaining flight distance budget across route segments, and optimizes dynamically feasible B-spline trajectories for local exploration. During execution, UAV measurements update a grid-based belief map, and the remaining trajectory is replanned when new information and the remaining budget justify adaptation. Across 48 scenario configurations, online replanning improves average KL reduction by 15.9% over the offline optimized planner and 48.6% over straight-line traversal.

</details>


### [68] [An Operator-Based Approach to STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28092)
*Panagiotis Rousseas,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可达性值函数算子的信号时序逻辑（STL）新方法，通过运算符嵌套规则直接处理复杂多嵌套公式，并实现在线控制综合，在理论和仿真中均证明了其表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的STL验证与控制综合方法在处理复杂嵌套公式时能力有限，亟需一种能够处理深层嵌套结构同时支持在线控制综合的新框架。

Method: 提出基于可达性值函数算子的STL新方法，通过开发算符嵌套规则（而非设计基于STL的可达性或控制屏障函数）来处理复杂多嵌套公式，并建立必要且充分的公式满足条件。

Result: 理论上提取了STL公式满足的必要和充分条件；在包含复杂片段的仿真中验证了方法的表达能力。

Conclusion: 该算子框架为处理复杂多嵌套STL公式提供了新的理论工具，同时支持在线控制综合，拓展了STL在自主规划与控制中的应用能力。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL), has recently seen extensive development, owing to its rich expressivenes for autonomous planning and control. Nevertheless, existing verification and control synthesis methods are limited with respect to the complexity and degree of nesting of the formulae. In this work, we propose a novel approach to STL based on an operator acting on reachability value functions. This constitutes a new theoretical framework for handling complex multi-nested formulae while at the same time providing tools for on-line control synthesis. In contrast to focusing on the design of STL-based reachability (or control barrier) functions, we develop operator-based nesting rules directly. Our method's expressiveness is demonstrated both theoretically, where necessary and sufficient conditions for STL formula satisfaction are extracted, as well as in simulations with complex fragments.

</details>


### [69] [STR Robot: Design of an Autonomous Mobile Robot from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28110)
*Vinh Nguyen,Gia-Uy Le,Tien-Dat Nguyen,Tri-Tin Nguyen,Vinh-Hao Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）框架的自主移动机器人实现方法，重点开发了机载控制、自定位和自主导航系统，先在仿真中验证再部署到真实平台，实验结果验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用仿真工具的高效性，在真实部署前加速自主机器人系统的开发与验证，降低实际测试的风险和成本。

Method: 基于现有机械平台，开发机载感知与计算模块实现位姿估计与自主导航；整体框架先在仿真环境中开发测试，再迁移至真实机器人进行实验评估。

Result: 仿真到现实的迁移成功，实验结果证明了所提方法的可行性，表明仿真为开发可靠的自主移动机器人系统提供了有效基础。

Conclusion: 仿真环境能够为真实世界中的自主移动机器人系统开发提供可靠的基础，Sim-to-Real方法在机器人领域中具有实用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid development of simulation tools, the development and validation of autonomous robotic systems have become more efficient before real-world deployment. This paper presents a simulation-to-real implementation of an autonomous mobile robot based on an existing mechanical platform. Instead of focusing on mechanical design, our work concentrates on the development of the onboard control, self-localization, and autonomous navigation system. The proposed robot is equipped with onboard sensing and computation to estimate its pose and navigate autonomously in the environment. The overall framework is first developed and tested in simulation, and then deployed on the real robot for experimental evaluation. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and show that simulation provides an effective foundation for developing reliable autonomous mobile robot systems. The source code will be released at https://ntdathp.github.io/outdoor-robot-web.

</details>


### [70] [Provably Guaranteed Polytopic Uncertainty Quantification for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28172)
*Guangyang Zeng,Yulong Gao,Yuan Shen,Lingpeng Chen,Haoying Li,Guodong Shi,Junfeng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对3D-3D地标SLAM，提出了具有确定性保证的不确定性量化算法，使用多面体表示不确定性集，并结合保形预测提升实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键机器人应用中需要保证且实用的感知不确定性量化，现有方法缺乏正式保证、依赖严格假设或仅关注位姿估计而非完整SLAM流程。

Method: 提出三个UQ模块：前向UQ（建图）、后向UQ（位姿跟踪）和位姿复合；使用多面体表示不确定性集；结合保形预测校准测量不确定性。

Result: 仿真和实验表明算法兼具强理论保证和实际可用性。

Conclusion: 提出的多面体不确定性量化框架在保证SLAM感知确定性的同时，通过保形预测提升了实际部署可行性。

Abstract: In safety-critical robotics applications, guaranteed and practical uncertainty quantification (UQ) in perception is vital. Many existing works either offer no formal containment guarantee, rely on restrictive modeling assumptions, or focus only on pose estimation rather than a complete SLAM pipeline. This paper presents provably guaranteed UQ algorithms for 3D-3D landmark-based SLAM. The algorithms consist of three basic UQ modules: forward UQ for mapping, backward UQ for pose tracking, and pose compound. Each module produces a certified uncertainty set; when the input uncertainty bounds are deterministic, the output sets inherit deterministic guarantees, i.e., they provably contain the true poses and landmarks. Specifically, we use polytopes to represent uncertainty sets, enabling tractable computations and a unified treatment of pose uncertainty. To enhance algorithms' practical usability, we incorporate conformal prediction to calibrate measurement uncertainty from data with prescribed probability. Simulations and experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms provide both strong theoretical guarantees and practical usability. The code is open-sourced at https://github.com/LIAS-CUHKSZ/Polytopic-SLAM-Uncertainty-Quantification.

</details>


### [71] [Natural Functional Gradients for Smooth Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28202)
*Kisang Park,Chanwoo Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自然函数梯度的轨迹优化框架，通过在函数空间中进行几何感知更新，生成无碰撞且平滑的机器人运动轨迹，在狭窄环境和高约束场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境和狭窄通道中，可行区域高度受限且碎片化，生成无碰撞和平滑的运动轨迹仍是机器人操作的核心挑战。

Method: 提出自然函数梯度轨迹优化框架：在函数空间中执行几何感知更新，使用高斯平滑的替代目标函数正则化优化景观，利用蒙特卡洛估计器进行黑箱轨迹评估，无需解析梯度。

Result: 在受限机器人操作任务中，该方法在轨迹可行性和运动平滑性方面优于代表性的规划和轨迹优化基线方法，尤其在几何间隙狭窄的环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: 自然函数梯度方法通过函数空间中的内蕴更新实现了轨迹正则化与时间离散化的解耦，为复杂约束环境下的运动规划提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating collision-free and smooth motions remains a central challenge in robotic manipulation, particularly in cluttered environments and narrow passages where feasible regions are highly constrained and fragmented. We propose a trajectory optimization framework that performs geometry-aware updates directly in function space using natural functional gradients. The method optimizes a Gaussian-smoothed surrogate objective that regularizes the optimization landscape through smooth trajectory perturbations while preserving trajectory-level structure. Because the updates are defined intrinsically in function space, trajectory regularity can be controlled independently of a particular time discretization. We derive a practical Monte-Carlo estimator of the natural functional gradient that requires only black-box trajectory evaluations, making the method applicable when analytic gradients are unavailable or unreliable due to collision checking and contact-rich simulation. Experiments on constrained robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method improves trajectory feasibility and produces smoother motions than representative planning and trajectory optimization baselines in environments with narrow geometric clearances. Additional results, videos, and implementation details are available at the project page: https://kisangpark.github.io/natural-functional-gradient/

</details>


### [72] [ProgVLA: Progress-Aware Robot Manipulation Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28231)
*Seungsu Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Seungmin Baek,Jean-Michel Renders*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProgVLA是一个紧凑的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过显式维护任务进度表示、两阶段Perceiver重采样机制和离线强化学习训练的进度头，在严格计算和内存预算下实现了可靠的长程机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在计算和内存预算受限的条件下处理长多模态序列时效率低、可靠性不足，尤其是在长程任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出ProgVLA模型，包含两个核心组件：1) 采用两阶段Perceiver重采样方案的多模态编码器，将可变长度的视觉、语言和本体感受流压缩为固定数量的控制上下文token；2) 使用离线强化学习目标训练的辅助进度头，联合学习归一化剩余视界目标的critic，提供任务进度内部估计并实现优势加权和成功加权的流匹配模仿学习。

Result: 在0.1B参数的ProgVLA模型上，在两项多任务机器人操作基准测试中取得了与更大规模预训练基线相当或更优的成功率，尤其在长程和困难任务上表现突出。消融实验表明学习到的上下文重采样器和任务自适应视觉微调贡献最大，进度感知训练在长程和多对象任务上提供额外增益。在真实玩具厨房环境中进一步验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: ProgVLA证明了通过紧凑设计结合显式进度建模，小模型可以在长程机器人操作任务上超越大规模基线，为资源受限场景下的机器人学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present ProgVLA, a compact vision-language-action (VLA) model designed for reliable robot manipulation under tight compute and memory budgets. The model specifically focuses on efficiently processing long multi-modal sequences by maintaining an explicit representation of task progress over extended horizons. To this end, ProgVLA integrates two key components. First, a multi-modal encoder with a two-stage Perceiver resampling scheme compresses variable-length visual, language, and proprioceptive streams into a fixed set of control-ready context tokens, substantially reducing sequence length while preserving cross-modal grounding. Second, an auxiliary set of progress heads is trained with offline reinforcement learning (RL) objectives to jointly learn critics over normalized remaining-horizon targets. This provides the policy with an internal estimate of task progress and enables advantage- and success-weighted flow-matching imitation learning. On two well-established multi-task robot manipulation benchmarks, a 0.1B-parameter ProgVLA model reaches success rates that are competitive with, and on long-horizon and harder task tiers exceed, substantially larger pretrained baselines. Ablations indicate that the learned context resampler and task-adaptive visual fine-tuning are the largest single contributors, while progress-aware training provides a consistent additional gain that is concentrated on long-horizon and multi-object tasks. We further validate the approach in real-world toy-kitchen environments.

</details>


### [73] [POINav: Benchmarking and Enhancing Final-Meters Arrival in Real-World Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28237)
*Ruiyan Gong,Meisheng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Mingchao Sun,Yanfen Shen,Zedong Chu,Zhining Gu,Wei Guo,Xiaolong Cheng,Qiming Li,Kangning Niu,Yanqing Zhu,Xiaolong Wu,Tianlun Li,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出POINav-Bench基准测试和POINav脑-行动框架，解决现实世界中基于兴趣点（POI）导航的"最后几米"问题。该基准包含11个商业区、126,398平方米、163个不同POI，使用3D高斯泼溅重建真实场景，并配合70K现实世界标识-入口数据集，实现高保真的闭环评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准在POI目标导航中面临粒度粗糙或由于生成场景导致的仿真-现实差距过大问题，难以评估真实世界中到达精确POI的"最后几米"导航能力。

Method: 1) 构建POINav-Bench：利用3DGS从真实捕获重建11个商业区，包含可遍历性标注和参考轨迹。2) 提出POINav脑-行动框架：脑模块进行POI接地推理，行动模块预测连续航点用于真实世界执行。3) 整理POINav-Dataset：包含70K真实世界标识-入口对。

Result: 实验表明所提出的框架为改进现实世界POI目标导航提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: POINav-Bench弥合了仿真与现实的差距，POINav脑-行动框架结合真实数据，有效推动了现实环境中精确POI导航的发展。

Abstract: Real-world navigation is fundamentally driven by Points of Interest (POIs), yet reaching a precise POI remains a critical "final-meters" challenge. Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks of POI-goal navigation often suffer from coarse granularity or significant sim-to-real gaps due to generated scene. To bridge this gap, we present POINav-Bench, the first benchmark designed for closed-loop evaluation of real-world POI-goal navigation. It comprises 11 commercial areas reconstructed from real-world captures using 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), covering 126,398 $m^{2}$ in total and spanning 163 distinct POIs. With traversability-aware annotations and reference trajectories, POINav-Bench enables high-fidelity evaluation of navigation agents in realistic, POI-rich real-world environments. Building on this, we propose the POINav Brain-Action Framework where a Brain module performs POI-grounded reasoning to guide an Action module in predicting continuous waypoints for real-world execution. We further curate the POINav-Dataset, containing 70K real-world signage-entrance pairs. Experiments show that our framework provides a viable path toward refining real-world POI-goal navigation.

</details>


### [74] [Natural Locomotion: Principle and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28254)
*Mirado Mortel,Luc Jaulin,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于被动动力学和能量交换原则的自然运动框架，通过“推进-振荡器交换功率”（POE功率）为零的条件定义自然运动流形（NLM），并在非完整约束系统中验证了该原理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人运动依赖预设轨迹控制，效率受限。本文旨在利用被动动力学、柔顺性和共振特性，提出一种无需外部能量输入、通过环境约束实现高效自然运动的普适性原理。

Method: 采用闭开构造方法：先关闭推进通道，揭示有效内部振荡器的标量作用-角结构或非线性模态扇区；再重新打开通道，重构位姿，确保接受周期满足内部复现和零平均POE功率条件。

Result: 在Chaplygin雪橇/摆驱动小车及其三体扩展两个理想非完整无滑移系统中验证了原理。标量情形下POE闭合等价于缺失的内部回归条件，可定理化计算NLM族。多自由度情形下需补充模态恒等、内部回归、动力学一致性等条件。

Conclusion: 自然运动本质上是一个设计问题：需要探究何种被动架构能够支持零个、一个或多个经过认证的NLM族。

Abstract: Robotic locomotion can become efficient when mechanisms exploit passive dynamics, compliance, and resonance rather than track prescribed trajectories. This paper formulates natural locomotion as an exchange principle for systems whose motion is mediated by environmental constraints or interactions. A motion is natural when an internal oscillator returns periodically, the body pose drifts, and the mean Propulsion--Oscillator Exchange power (POE power) vanishes over one cycle. The selected family is a Natural Locomotion Manifold (NLM). We develop the conservative realization of this principle for continuous ideal environmental constraints: the constraints do no external work, total mechanical energy is conserved, and zero mean POE power is an internal exchange with the environment-mediated propulsive channel, not external energy input.
  The method is a closed/open construction. The propulsive channel is first closed to reveal an effective internal oscillator, organized by scalar action-angle structure in one effective degree of freedom or by nonlinear modal sectors in several degrees of freedom. The channel is then reopened, pose is reconstructed, and accepted cycles must preserve internal recurrence and zero mean POE power.
  We demonstrate the principle on two ideal nonholonomic no-slip systems: a Chaplygin-sleigh / pendulum-driven car and a three-body extension. In the scalar case, POE closure is equivalent to the missing internal return condition, giving a theorem-backed computation of the NLM family. In the multi-degree case, POE closure remains necessary but must be completed by modal identity, internal return, dynamics consistency, same fixed passive architecture, and nonzero displacement. Natural locomotion becomes a design question: which passive architectures support no, one, or several certified NLM families?

</details>


### [75] [IMU Propagation as Preintegration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28279)
*Jianzhu Huai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明IMU预积分与IMU传播在数学上等价，提出可以通过包装现有IMU传播例程来获得预积分测量、偏置雅可比和协方差，反之亦然，从而简化代码复用并支持不同误差状态定义间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU预积分在因子图视觉-惯性、激光-惯性、雷达-惯性状态估计中被广泛使用，但常被视为与常规IMU传播分离的独立实现。作者希望揭示两者本质等价，从而简化实现和代码复用。

Method: 提出一种约定无关的视角，展示预积分测量、偏置雅可比和协方差可通过包装现有IMU传播例程获得；同时预积分模块也可反向恢复状态转移矩阵和传播协方差。通过RK4传播与GTSAM的正切/流形预积分模块进行对比实验验证。

Result: 随机IMU序列上的实验表明，基于RK4的传播实现与GTSAM的预积分模块在恢复的雅可比、协方差和状态转移矩阵上高度一致。

Conclusion: IMU预积分与传播是同一底层计算的不同实现方式，该视角可简化现有传播代码的复用，支持不同误差状态定义间的转换，并为预积分实现提供一致性校验手段。

Abstract: IMU preintegration is widely used in factor-graph-based visual--inertial, lidar--inertial, and radar--inertial state estimation, yet it is often treated as a specialized implementation separate from conventional IMU propagation. This note shows that IMU preintegration and propagation are equivalent realizations of the same underlying computation. We present a convention-agnostic view in which the preintegrated measurement, bias Jacobians, and covariance can be obtained by wrapping an existing IMU propagation routine, while a preintegration module can conversely recover state-transition matrices and propagated covariances. This perspective simplifies the reuse of existing propagation code, supports translation across different error-state definitions, and provides practical consistency checks for preintegration implementations. Experiments with random IMU sequences demonstrate close agreement between an RK4-based propagation implementation and GTSAM's tangent and manifold preintegration modules in the recovered Jacobians, covariances, and transition matrices.

</details>


### [76] [EventShiftFlow: Towards Hardware-efficient FPGA-based Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28312)
*Arianna Alonso Bizzi,Fernando Cladera,C. J. Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于事件相机的流式速度估计器，将异步事件离散化为固定时长的时间片，构建1比特空间占用网格，并行的评估多个速度假设，仅使用整数逻辑（移位寄存器、计数器、比较器、LUT乘法）无需除法器、DSP、浮点运算或帧重建。在噪声合成数据上能恢复速度和方向，实际事件相机序列方向准确率达99.5%，存储需求<2kB，并在Xilinx Artix-7上实现了单轴原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机运动估计方法计算密集，难以映射到FPGA硬件，不适用于低延迟、SWaP受限的机器人平台。

Method: 将异步事件离散化为固定时间片，构建1比特空间占用网格，并行评估多个速度假设，仅使用移位寄存器、计数器、比较器和LUT映射乘法等固定宽度整数逻辑。

Result: 噪声合成数据上能恢复速度大小和方向；真实事件相机序列方向准确率达99.5%；在占用密度10-40%范围内保持鲁棒；存储需求<2kB；在低成本的Xilinx Artix-7上实现了单轴原型。

Conclusion: 该方法以稀疏、量化的速度估计替代稠密亚像素光流，适用于低延迟反应式避障等任务，特别适合SWaP受限平台。

Abstract: Event-based vision sensors offer asynchronous, high-temporal-resolution measurements that are attractive for low-latency robotic perception, but many event-based motion estimation methods are computationally intensive and difficult to map to FPGA hardware. We present a streaming velocity estimator that discretizes asynchronous events into fixed-duration time bins, constructs a 1-bit spatial occupancy grid, and evaluates multiple velocity hypotheses in parallel using only fixed-width integer logic - shift registers, counters, comparators, and small LUT-mapped multiplies - with no dividers and no DSP blocks. It requires no frame reconstruction, no floating-point arithmetic, and no iterative optimization. The method deliberately trades dense sub-pixel optical flow for a sparse, quantized velocity estimate at each active pixel, suited to low-latency tasks such as reactive obstacle avoidance on size-, weight-, and power-constrained platforms. On noisy synthetic data with known ground-truth velocities, the method recovers both magnitude and direction, with magnitude estimates being most challenged when objects of different velocities intersect. On a real event-camera sequence, directional accuracy reaches 99.5% across all four evaluated motion segments, with performance remaining robust across occupancy densities in the 10-40% range. We characterize the algorithm's density-dependent behavior, present a parameter sensitivity analysis, show that the proposed datapath requires less than 2 kB of storage, and implement a single-axis prototype on a low-cost Xilinx Artix-7.

</details>


### [77] [Chance-Constrained MPPI under State and Dynamic Object Prediction Uncertainty and the Evaluation of Collision Risk Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28330)
*Benjamin Serfling,Konrad Doll,Kati Radkhah-Lens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于机会约束的MPPI控制在导航中因上游估计器校准不良导致的闭环失效问题，提出了一种结合严格评估方法（proper scoring rules）和新型规划架构（DUCCT-MPPI）的解决方案。DUCCT-MPPI通过单管无迹变换融合定位不确定性，并通过蒙特卡洛聚合处理动态障碍物预测不确定性，在密集环境中导航成功率提升约28%，同时实现最低旅行时间和最小社会力干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机会约束MPPI控制假设定位与感知的不确定性校准良好，但实际中估计器常存在校准偏差，导致过置信引发安全隐患、欠置信引发过度保守或概率稀释。现有方法缺乏对闭环执行中碰撞风险预测统计有效性的系统评估框架。

Method: 1) 提出基于proper scoring rules的严格评估方法，用于检验闭环执行中碰撞风险预测的统计有效性；2) 提出DUCCT-MPPI实时风险感知规划架构，通过单管无迹变换（UT）近似融合定位不确定性，并通过蒙特卡洛聚合融合动态障碍物预测不确定性。

Result: 在密集环境中，DUCCT-MPPI相比蒙特卡洛MPPI基线，导航成功率提升近28%，同时实现最低旅行时间和最小化诱导社会力。

Conclusion: 可靠的自主导航概率安全性不仅需要表达性强的风险模型，更需要整个自主堆栈中统计有效的置信度估计。

Abstract: Chance-constrained Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is increasingly adopted for navigation in dynamic environments to explicitly bound collision risk. However, these probabilistic guarantees implicitly assume that upstream uncertainties from localization and perception are well-calibrated. In practice, estimators are often miscalibrated, inducing characteristic closed-loop failure modes: overconfidence leads to systematic safety violations, while underconfidence triggers overly conservative freezing or probability dilution. To address this critical gap, our primary contribution is a rigorous evaluation methodology applying proper scoring rules to assess the statistical validity of predicted collision risks during closed-loop execution. Concurrently, Dual-Uncertainty Chance-Constrained Tube MPPI (DUCCT-MPPI) is proposed as a real-time, risk-aware planning architecture. DUCCT-MPPI jointly integrates localization uncertainty via a one-tube Unscented Transform (UT) approximation and dynamic obstacle prediction uncertainty via Monte Carlo aggregation. Through extensive physics-based simulations, the framework demonstrates robust failure-mitigation, seamlessly transitioning to safe, conservative maneuvering without succumbing to functional deadlocks in highly cluttered environments. In highly cluttered environments, DUCCT-MPPI achieves superior robustness, outperforming established Monte Carlo MPPI baselines by nearly 28\% in navigation success rate, while simultaneously recording the lowest travel times and minimizing induced social forces. Ultimately, these findings establish that reliable probabilistic safety in autonomous navigation dictates not only expressive risk models but statistically valid uncertainty estimates throughout the entire autonomy stack.

</details>


### [78] [Safety-Critical Adaptive Impedance Control via Nonsmooth Control Barrier Functions under State and Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28367)
*Faisal Lawan,Xiaoran Han,Joaquin Carrasco,Barry Lennox,Xiaoxiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线自适应阻抗控制框架，结合基于二次规划的安全滤波器和新型组合位置-速度非光滑控制障碍函数（NCBF），在不确定动力学条件下实现关节级安全约束与柔顺交互，并通过区间二型模糊系统补偿未知动力学，利用扰动观测器增强鲁棒性，经7自由度机械臂仿真验证了安全约束满足与鲁棒阻抗跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人-人交互及接触丰富的任务中，不确定性、外力及执行器限制会同时降低机器人操作的安全性和性能，因此需要一种能够同时保证安全约束和柔顺交互的控制方法。

Method: 1) 基于二次规划的安全滤波器与组合位置-速度非光滑控制障碍函数(NCBF)相结合，将关节位置和速度约束统一为相对度为一的障碍函数约束；2) 采用区间二型模糊逻辑系统在线补偿未知动力学；3) 通过软约束与精确罚函数恢复处理执行器力矩限制；4) 引入扰动观测器增强安全机制以应对建模误差和外部交互力。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂上，针对严重参数不确定性和外部交互力/力矩的仿真实验，验证了所提方法能够满足安全约束并实现鲁棒的阻抗跟踪。

Conclusion: 通过复合李雅普诺夫分析，证明了安全集的前向不变性和阻抗跟踪误差的一致最终有界性，表明该方法在理论上和实践上均能保障安全交互与鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Safe physical interaction is critical for deploying robotic manipulators in human-robot interaction and contact-rich tasks, where uncertainty, external forces, and actuator limitations can compromise both performance and safety. We propose an online adaptive impedance control framework that enforces joint-state safety while achieving compliant interaction under uncertain dynamics. The approach combines a quadratic-program-based safety filter with a novel composed position-velocity non-smooth control barrier function (NCBF), enabling joint position and velocity constraints to be enforced through a unified relative-degree-one barrier. Unknown dynamics are compensated online using an interval type-2 fuzzy logic system, while actuator torque limits are handled through soft constraints with exact penalty recovery of feasible solutions. A disturbance-observer-enhanced safety mechanism improves robustness against modelling errors and external interaction forces. Using composite Lyapunov analysis, we prove forward invariance of the safe set and the uniform ultimately boundedness of the impedance-tracking error. Simulations on a 7-DOF manipulator with severe parametric uncertainty and external interaction wrenches demonstrate safe constraint satisfaction and robust impedance tracking.

</details>


### [79] [A Digital Twin Framework for Virtual Visuo-Haptic Teleoperation of Complex-Shaped Optical Microrobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28448)
*Zongcai Tan,Lan Wei,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向复杂形状光驱动微机器人的数字孪生框架，集成视觉-触觉遥操作、多球分布式操控模型和光学力估计，在模拟细胞递送任务中触觉反馈使接触力标准差降低53.2%、微机器人-光阱中心距离标准差降低55.2%，任务成功率从30%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光镊虽能提供皮牛级精密操控，但针对复杂形状光驱动微机器人的视觉-触觉遥操作框架尚不成熟，特别是在多光阱操控场景中缺乏有效的力反馈和稳定性提示机制。

Method: 构建数字孪生框架，集成基于图像的位姿与深度估计、微机器人运动仿真、基于模型的多球分布式操控力建模与光学力估计，并在ROS连接的双臂遥操作系统中实现触觉渲染。

Result: 触觉反馈使接触力标准差降低53.2%，微机器人-光阱中心距离标准差降低55.2%，任务成功率从30%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生框架能有效评估复杂形状光驱动微机器人的视觉-触觉遥操作策略，触觉反馈显著提升了操控精度和任务成功率。

Abstract: Optical tweezers (OT) provide piconewton-scale manipulation for delicate biomedical tasks, where visuo-haptic feedback can improve operator awareness by conveying interaction-force cues and trap-stability information. However, visuo-haptic teleoperation frameworks for complex-shaped optical microrobots remain underdeveloped, particularly in multi-trap manipulation scenarios. This paper presents a digital twin framework for virtual visuo-haptic teleoperation of complex-shaped OT-driven microrobots. The framework integrates a digital twin environment, image-based pose and depth estimation, microrobot motion simulation, and model-based haptic rendering within a Robot Operating System (ROS)-connected bimanual teleoperation system. For force modeling, we combine a Multi-Sphere Distributed Manipulation (MSDM) model with optical-force estimation from the Optical Tweezers Toolbox, enabling simulator-driven visuo-haptic feedback. The framework reproduces representative microrobot motion trends and provides haptic force rendering that is numerically consistent with the fitted optical-force model. In simulated cell-delivery tasks, haptic feedback reduced the standard deviations of the contact-force metric and the microrobot-to-trap-center distance metric by 53.2% and 55.2%, respectively, and improved task success from 30% to 80%. These results demonstrate the framework's effectiveness for evaluating visuo-haptic teleoperation strategies for complex-shaped optical microrobots.

</details>


### [80] [Learning a Kinodynamic Trajectory Manifold for Impact-Aware Compliant Catching of Fast-Moving Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28462)
*Guorui Pei,Mengshi Zhang,Xi Chen,Jinsong Wu,Jiaming Qi,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习和低维流形学习的快速自由飞行物体抓取方法，通过在仿真中学习抓取轨迹，实现无需在线优化的实时物体抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由飞行物体的快速抓取面临反应时间短、冲击不确定性和运动动力学约束等挑战，传统方法难以同时满足实时性和鲁棒性要求。

Method: 在仿真中使用强化学习收集成功抓取轨迹，学习低维运动动力学轨迹流形；运行时直接将估计的物体初始状态映射到参考抓取轨迹，无需在线非线性优化；通过接触附近的柔顺控制跟踪轨迹以改善冲击吸收和捕获稳定性。

Result: 实现了无需在线优化的实时物体抓取，降低了计算复杂度，提高了对冲击的吸收能力和抓取稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了快速飞行物体抓取中的实时性与鲁棒性问题，通过离线学习和柔顺控制策略实现了高效稳定的抓取。

Abstract: Fast catching of free-flying objects is difficult because of short reaction time, impact uncertainty, and kinodynamic constraints. We use reinforcement learning in simulation to collect successful catching trajectories and learn a low-dimensional kinodynamic trajectory manifold. At run time, the estimated object initial state is mapped directly to a reference catching trajectory without online nonlinear optimization. The trajectory is tracked with compliant control near contact for improved impact absorption and capture stability.

</details>


### [81] [EIT-Pneumatic Hybrid Robotic Skin for Practical and Accurate Force Map Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28468)
*Junhwi Cho,Sunggyu Bae,Junghyeon Ma,Hyosang Lee,Jung Kim,Kyungseo Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合电阻抗成像（EIT）与气动弹性的混合机器人皮肤，通过3D打印和喷涂工艺制造，利用Tikhonov正则化反演与气动校准实现大面积触觉传感，将力重建灵敏度不均匀性系数从0.31降至0.14，并在人形机器人胸部集成验证了多触点场景下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决EIT触觉传感中长期以来存在的灵敏度不均匀性问题，实现低成本、易制造、可扩展的机器人全身触觉传感。

Method: 采用3D打印与喷涂法制备混合机器人皮肤；结合EIT（电阻抗成像）与气动触觉传感；使用Tikhonov正则化反演算法进行力重建，并配合逐个气垫的校准方案。

Result: 负载压头实验表明同一气垫内不同位置力重建一致性良好；灵敏度不均匀性系数从EIT-only的0.31降至0.14；在人形机器人胸部集成测试中，气动信号在多触点接触场景下保持可靠。

Conclusion: 该混合方案为实际机器人系统实现准确、可扩展的全身触觉传感提供了一条可行路径，有效克服了EIT灵敏度不均匀这一长期局限。

Abstract: We present a hybrid robotic skin that combines electrical impedance tomography (EIT) with pneumatic tactile sensing to improve force reconstruction capability. The developed robotic skin is fabricated entirely by 3D printing and spray coating, making it affordable and easy to build. A Tikhonov-regularized inverse reconstruction, paired with per-pad pneumatic calibration, enables accurate large-area tactile sensing with a simple measurement scheme. For validation, we conducted load-cell indentation experiments; the results showed consistent force reconstruction across locations within a pad. Compared with an EIT-only baseline, sensitivity non-uniformity was also reduced, with the coefficient of variation decreasing from 0.31 to 0.14, indicating that the proposed approach addresses a longstanding limitation of EIT. We further demonstrated chest-mounted integration on a humanoid robot and found that the pneumatic signals remained reliable across diverse contact scenarios, including multiple simultaneous contacts on the same sensing pad. These results indicate a practical path toward accurate, scalable whole-body tactile sensing in real robotic systems.

</details>


### [82] [What Frozen VLAs Already Know About Success: A Probing Study of Value-Like Structure in Foundation Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28527)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junnan Nie,Junyi Lao,Wei Cheng,Chenghao Liu,Jiaxin Jiang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然仅通过模仿动作进行训练（损失函数不涉及奖励或成功估计），但其冻结的表示中编码了关于任务成功的信息。通过轻量线性探针，可以从这些特征中恢复蒙特卡洛结果目标，并且在same-task、same-timestep条件下，Pi0.5探针达到约92%的配对排序准确率。将该探针用作测试时选择器，Pi0.5在push-plate任务上的成功率从26.7%（贪心解码）提升至44.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLA策略在仅通过模仿学习（动作克隆）训练后，其冻结的隐层表示是否编码了超出训练目标之外的、关于任务成功/失败的信息，并验证能否利用这些信息改善策略性能。

Method: 从LIBERO-Goal数据集的混合成功与失败轨迹中，使用轻量线性探针在冻结的VLA特征（OpenVLA、Pi0.5、DINOv2、CLIP）上预测蒙特卡洛结果目标；通过same-task、same-timestep匹配比较排除任务和时间捷径；将探针作为测试时选择器，对Pi0.5采样的动作前缀进行排序选择。

Result: Pi0.5探针在配对排序准确率达到约92%（标签打乱控制组为随机水平）；push-plate任务成功率从26.7%（贪心解码）提升至44.3%，wine-rack任务也有正向提升；但增益并非普遍适用且需要额外推理计算。

Conclusion: 冻结的VLA策略虽然仅通过模仿目标训练，但其表示中已隐式编码了关于任务成功的信息，这种信息可被轻量探针读取并用于引导动作选择，改善策略性能。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) policies are trained to imitate actions; their loss never asks them to estimate reward, progress, or future success. Their frozen representations nevertheless carry such information, and it can be read out and used to guide action choice without retraining the policy. From mixed successful and failed manipulation trajectories on LIBERO-Goal, we recover Monte-Carlo outcome targets using lightweight linear probes on frozen features. The targets are consistently predictable from OpenVLA, Pi0.5, DINOv2, and CLIP features, and substantially less so from baselines built on progress, time-to-go, task identity, or proprioception. To rule out task and temporal shortcuts, we evaluate the probes under same-task, same-timestep matched comparisons: Pi0.5 probes still reach roughly 92% pairwise ordering accuracy, while label-shuffled controls stay at chance. Used as a test-time selector over sampled Pi0.5 action prefixes, the same probe turns this offline finding into behavior: on push-plate, success rises from 26.7% under greedy decoding to 44.3%, with a second positive case on wine-rack. The gains are not universal and require additional inference compute, but the underlying finding is clean: frozen VLAs already encode information about success that their imitation objective never explicitly demands.

</details>


### [83] [SPRINT: Efficient Spectral Priors for Humanoid Athletic Sprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28549)
*Yantong Wei,Kaihong Huang,Hainan Pan,Jiawei Luo,Jiawei Zhou,Ziyan Mai,Zhiwen Zeng,Yaonan Wang,Huimin Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRINT框架，利用频率自适应谱先验，仅需5个参考序列即可生成高速仿人运动轨迹，在Unitree G1平台上实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，达到6m/s峰值速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人高速奔跑面临两大障碍：缺乏适合人形机器人的运动学参考数据，以及现有框架无法在奔跑过程中保持稳定性。

Method: 通过频率域表征人类运动的基本周期性，利用5个离散运动序列的参考库生成频率自适应谱先验，该先验能在广泛速度范围内生成运动学可行的关节轨迹，并成功外推到超出参考分布的速度。

Result: 在Unitree G1平台上实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，达到6m/s峰值奔跑速度，展现出无缝步态转换，同时保持仿生自然性。

Conclusion: 频率自适应谱先验可作为人形机器人运动奔跑的高数据效率基础。

Abstract: The pursuit of humanoid athletic sprints is hindered by a scarcity of humanoid-viable kinematic reference data and the inability of existing frameworks to maintain stability during sprints. To overcome these limitations, we introduce SPRINT, a novel framework driven by efficient, frequency-adaptive spectral priors. By characterizing the fundamental periodicity of human locomotion in the frequency domain using a reference library of five discrete motion sequences, these priors generate kinematically feasible joint trajectories across a broad velocity spectrum, successfully extrapolating to speeds that exceed the reference distribution. Guided by these pretrained priors, the SPRINT policy achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer in field experiments on the Unitree G1 platform, reaching a peak sprinting velocity of 6 m/s and demonstrating seamless gait transitions while preserving biomimetic naturalness. Ultimately, this work establishes frequency-adaptive spectral priors as a highly data-efficient foundation for humanoid athletic sprints. The project page is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/SPRINT-138A/.

</details>


### [84] [SARAD: LLM-Based Safety-Aware Hybrid Reinforcement Learning with Collision Prediction for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28583)
*Kangyu Wu,Peng Cui,Guoxi Chen,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SARAD是一种融合大语言模型(LLM)和深度强化学习(DRL)的安全感知混合框架，通过检索增强生成(RAG)指导的探索替代DRL的随机探索，利用注意力判别器整合LLM先验知识，并设计碰撞预测器提升自动驾驶的安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRL存在不安全随机探索和收敛缓慢的问题，而LLM在实时推理中存在固有延迟，需要一种兼顾安全与效率的自动驾驶决策框架。

Method: 1) 用RAG增强的LLM引导决策替代DRL的随机探索；2) 提出注意力判别器将LLM先验知识整合到DRL策略优化中；3) 设计基于历史碰撞数据微调的碰撞预测器。

Result: 在Highway-Env模拟器上的大量实验表明，SARAD在自动驾驶任务中取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: SARAD通过LLM与DRL的协同混合框架，有效解决了自动驾驶中安全性与效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Ensuring both safety and efficiency in decision-making for autonomous driving systems remains a fundamental challenge. Traditional Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) suffers from unsafe random exploration and slow convergence, while Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate inherent latency in real-time inference operations. To address these limitations, this paper proposes SARAD, a novel safety-aware hybrid framework that synergizes LLMs and DRL for autonomous driving. SARAD substitutes the random exploration of DRL with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-enhanced, LLM-guided decisions sourced from a dynamic expert knowledge repository. An attention discriminator is proposed to integrate the prior knowledge of LLMs into DRL policy optimization. A collision predictor module, fine-tuned with historical collision data, is further designed to improve vehicle safety. Extensive experiments show that SARAD achieves significant performance improvements in the Highway-Env simulator, validating the effectiveness of the proposed model in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [85] [PrimitiveVLA: Learning Reusable Motion Primitives for Efficient and Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28634)
*Yutai Li,Shaohui Peng,Jiaming Guo,Di Huang,Zihao Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Yunkai Gao,Siming Lan,Ling Li,Xing Hu,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrimitiveVLA提出了一种以基元为中心的拆解与组装范式，通过多模态规范表征将演示数据拆解为可复用的运动基元，并在推理阶段使用VLM规划器和LLM生成的开关节模块进行闭环执行，显著提升了VLA模型的数据效率和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型采用直接的指令到控制映射范式，导致模型被迫记忆完整的任务轨迹而非可复用的运动模式，造成数据效率低下和泛化能力差。作者旨在通过引入基元视角来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出PrimitiveVLA框架，包含两个阶段：(1)微调阶段拆解——利用自动化流水线将演示数据拆解为可复用基元，并基于共享的MCR进行表征；(2)推理阶段组装——使用VLM规划器进行任务级规划，配合LLM生成的开关节模块实现鲁棒的闭环执行。

Result: 实验表明该框架提升了数据效率，在未见任务和长时域任务上实现了优越的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过将任务拆解为可复用的基元，使VLA模型学习不变的运动模式而非任务特定的轨迹，从而有效解决了数据效率和泛化问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for generalist robotic policies, yet their adaptation is hindered by data inefficiency and poor generalization. We argue that these bottlenecks stem from the prevailing Direct Instruction-to-Control Mapping, which forces models to memorize monolithic trajectories rather than reusable motion patterns, i.e., primitives. We propose PrimitiveVLA, a framework that shifts this paradigm toward a Primitive-Centric Disassemble & Assemble paradigm. Supported by a shared Multimodal Canonical Representation (MCR), PrimitiveVLA unifies two phases: (1) Fine-tuning-phase Disassembly, which uses an automated pipeline to disassemble demonstrations into reusable primitives; and (2) Inference-phase Assembly, which employs a VLM-based planner and an LLM-generated switch module for robust closed-loop execution. By disassembling tasks into reusable primitives, PrimitiveVLA enables VLA models to learn invariant motion patterns instead of task-specific trajectories. Extensive experiments show that our framework improves data efficiency and achieves superior zero-shot generalization across unseen and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [86] [How VLAs Fail Differently: Black-Box Action Monitoring Reveals Architecture-Specific Failure Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28726)
*Krishnam Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA架构在电机命令层面存在根本性的不同失败模式：方向反转率是通用的失败预测指标（AUROC达0.93），而速度违规监控虽广泛使用却无预测价值。离散token架构与连续架构需要不同的监控器，架构匹配的监控选择至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA部署代码中最常用的安全机制是速度检查，但缺乏对不同VLA架构失败模式的系统性量化研究，导致可能使用了无效的监控手段。

Method: 在PushT和ALOHA 14自由度双臂操作任务上，对VQ-BeT、Diffusion Policy和ACT三种VLA架构执行n=450个episode的相同评估协议，分析方向反转率、急动度监控和速度违规等指标的预测能力。

Result: (1) 方向反转率是通用失败预测器，AUROC为0.93/0.79/0.91；(2) 急动度监控仅对离散token架构有效（AUROC梯度0.88/0.69/0.41）；(3) 速度违规在所有架构上均无预测性（AUROC 0.41-0.69）；(4) 连续家族VLA的速度监控几乎无信号（ACT: 0.52, Diffusion: 0.41）。

Conclusion: 离散与连续VLA家族产生性质不同的失败特征，需要不同的监控器；没有通用监控器，必须进行架构匹配的监控选择。该发现由训练无关、黑盒、带保形校准的SafeContract工具支撑。

Abstract: We discover that VLA architectures fail in fundamentally different, predictable ways at the motor-command level. Running VQ-BeT, Diffusion Policy, and ACT on identical evaluation protocols (n=450 episodes across PushT and ALOHA 14-DOF bimanual manipulation), we find: (1) direction reversal rate is a universal failure predictor across all three architectures (AUROC=0.93, 0.79, 0.91; p<0.001); (2) jerk monitoring is predictive only for discrete-token architectures, following a discrete-to-continuous gradient (0.88, 0.69, 0.41); (3) velocity violations alone are non-predictive everywhere (AUROC 0.41-0.69), yet velocity checking is the most common safety mechanism in VLA deployment code; and (4) for continuous-family VLAs, velocity monitoring provides effectively zero predictive signal (AUROC=0.52 on ACT, 0.41 on Diffusion), proving that architecture-matched monitor selection is essential. These results quantify a monitoring consequence of the well-known discrete/continuous VLA distinction: the two families produce qualitatively different failure signatures that require different monitors. No single monitor works universally; architecture-matched selection is required. This finding was enabled by SafeContract, a training-free, black-box action monitoring toolkit with conformal calibration. Code: https://github.com/krishnam94/vla-edge

</details>


### [87] [Imitation Learning for Robot Assistance in Open Surgery: A Multi-Policy Evaluation on Suture Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28736)
*Xucheng Wang,Zhizhou Yang,Xiaoman Zhang,Sung Eun Kim,Romain Hardy,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次评估了通用模仿学习在开放手术中实现外科医生-机器人协作辅助的能力，聚焦于缝合跟随任务。研究收集了160次遥操作演示（32,374帧），对四种模仿学习策略（ACT、Diffusion Policy、SmolVLA、π₀）进行了基准测试。在理想条件下，四种策略达到50-75%的任务成功率，深度误差是主要失败模式。其中π₀凭借预训练的视觉-语言骨干取得最强结果，在数据效率、背景变化鲁棒性和轨迹平滑度上表现优异，在外科医生-机器人缝合试验中实现了92%的缝合完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用模仿学习在开放手术中对机器人助手进行协作辅助的可行性，特别是缝合跟随这一重复性关键动作，以减轻外科医生负担并提升手术效率和一致性。

Method: 收集160次遥操作演示（32,374帧），基于开源机器人臂，对四种架构各异的模仿学习策略（ACT、Diffusion Policy、SmolVLA、π₀）进行训练和评估，沿数据集大小、相机视角和背景变化三个临床相关维度设置32种配置测试28个模型，并进行外科医生-机器人缝合试验。

Result: 理想条件下四种策略任务成功率为50-75%，深度误差是主导失败模式。π₀表现最优，数据效率更高、对背景变化鲁棒性更强、轨迹更平滑，在真实缝合试验中达到92%的缝合完成率。

Conclusion: 开放手术中的协作机器人辅助可作为模仿学习的可行目标，深度感知和末端执行器设计是临床转化的关键优先事项。

Abstract: This study presents the first evaluation of general-purpose imitation learning for surgeon-robot collaborative assistance in open surgery, targeting suture following: the grab-pull-release motion an assistant performs at every stitch. We collect 160 teleoperated demonstrations (32,374 frames) on an open-source robot arm, benchmark four architecturally diverse imitation learning policies (ACT, Diffusion Policy, SmolVLA, $π_0$) across 28 trained models evaluated in 32 configurations along three clinically motivated dimensions: dataset size, camera viewpoint, and background variation. Our results demonstrate that under ideal conditions, the four policies achieve $50$-$75\%$ task success, with depth error as the dominant failure mode across all architectures. Among all policies, $π_0$ achieves the strongest results with a pretrained vision-language backbone, demonstrating superior data efficiency, greater robustness to background variation, and smoother trajectories compatible with surgical workflow. When deployed in a surgeon-robot suturing trial, $π_0$ yields a $92\%$ stitch completion rate. These findings establish collaborative robotic assistance in open surgery as a feasible target for imitation learning and highlight depth perception and end-effector design as key priorities for clinical translation.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Binary: Sim-to-Real Dexterous Manipulation with Physics-Grounded Contact Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28812)
*Jiahe Pan,Stelian Coros,Jitendra Malik,Toru Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoP（压力中心）作为触觉表示，通过可微动力学校准方案实现从仿真到真实的零样本迁移，在插销入孔和球平衡任务中优于粗粒度触觉基线，并发现策略能涌现学习物体质量等物理属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉丰富的操作任务中，真实数据收集困难；现有sim-to-real方法将触觉数据简化为粗粒度低维特征，丢失了复杂操作所需的信息密度。

Method: 提出基于物理原理的CoP触觉表示，保留稠密接触信息且对sim-to-real迁移鲁棒；设计基于可微动力学的传感器校准方案，无需真实力测量即可估计触觉单元朝向。

Result: 在插销入孔和球平衡任务中，CoP策略实现多指手的零样本sim-to-real迁移，并优于二进制接触和原始触觉单元基线。策略状态分析表明CoP条件策略能编码物体质量等任务相关物理属性。

Conclusion: CoP作为一种物理原理驱动的触觉表示，有效弥合了仿真-现实间隙，为复杂接触丰富的操作任务提供可行的sim-to-real方案。

Abstract: A primary bottleneck in contact-rich manipulation is the difficulty of collecting real-world data. Sim-to-real reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative, but the simulation-reality gap prevents information-dense modalities like touch from being effectively used. Existing sim-to-real methods often mitigate this gap by simplifying tactile data into coarse low-dimensional features -- sacrificing the richness required for complex manipulation. In this work, we introduce Center-of-Pressure (CoP), an effective tactile representation grounded in physical principles that preserves dense contact information while maintaining robustness for sim-to-real transfer. To support this representation, we propose a sensor calibration scheme based on differentiable dynamics, enabling the estimation of taxel orientations without requiring ground-truth force measurements. We evaluate CoP on two blind, challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks: peg-in-hole insertion and ball balancing. Across both tasks, policies conditioned on CoP achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a multi-fingered hand, and outperform both coarse binary-contact and raw-taxel baselines. Analysis of learned policy states further suggests that CoP-conditioned policies encode task-relevant physical properties, such as object mass, as an emergent byproduct of control.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [A higher homological approach to the $q$-characters of representations of quantum affine algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28682)
*Élie Casbi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为任意无多重边的无圈箭图Q构造了一个幺半范畴R_Q，其不可分解对象是Q的路代数上有限维模的张量积。证明了具有良好同调性质的特殊链复形（高几乎分裂复形）的存在性和唯一性（同伦意义下），且该性质在R_Q中对象张量作用下保持。通过建立mod kQ中一簇完备例外序列作为关键工具，进一步证明了当Q具有高度函数时，这些复形的欧拉示性数与Hernandez-Leclerc范畴C^(1)中标准模的截断q-特征标一致。当Q的底图为A_n型Dynkin图时，还将有限型簇代数A_Q中所有簇变量的簇特征标解释为R_Q中某些链复形的欧拉示性数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意无多重边的无圈箭图构造一个范畴框架，在其中可系统地研究高几乎分裂复形，并建立其与量子群表示论（Hernandez-Leclerc范畴）及簇代数中簇特征标的深层联系。

Method: 通过构造幺半范畴R_Q（对象为路代数上有限维模的张量积），在其中定义并证明高几乎分裂复形的存在唯一性；建立mod kQ中完备例外序列簇；引入高度函数并计算欧拉示性数；在A_n型Dynkin情形下与簇代数中的簇特征标建立对应。

Result: (1) 构造了幺半范畴R_Q；(2) 证明了高几乎分裂复形的存在唯一性及张量保持性；(3) 建立了完备例外序列族；(4) 当Q有高度函数时，欧拉示性数与截断q-特征标一致；(5) 在A_n型Dynkin情形下，簇代数中所有簇变量的簇特征标可解释为R_Q中链复形的欧拉示性数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了箭图表示论、量子群表示论和簇代数之间的新联系，为高几乎分裂复形提供了统一的构造框架，揭示了这三个领域中核心不变量之间的深刻对应关系。

Abstract: For any acyclic quiver $Q$ without multiple edges, we construct a monoidal category $\mathcal{R}_Q$ whose indecomposable objects are tensor products (over the base field) of finite-dimensional modules over the path algebra of $Q$. We show the existence and uniqueness up to homotopy of certain distinguished chain complexes satisfying good homological properties (higher almost split complexes) preserved under tensoring by objects in $\mathcal{R}_Q$. As a crucial ingredient for this construction, we establish the existence of a family of complete exceptional sequences in $\mathrm{mod}\,\mathbf{k}Q$ satisfying many good properties, which we believe might be of independent interest. We then prove that when $Q$ admits a height function, the Euler characteristics of (the images under certain additive functor of) these complexes coincide with the truncated $q$-characters of the standard modules in Hernandez-Leclerc's category $\mathcal{C}^{(1)}$. Applying our results to the case where the underlying graph of $Q$ is a Dynkin diagram of type $A_n, n \geq 1$, we also interpret the cluster characters of all cluster variables in the finite type cluster algebra $\mathcal{A}_Q$ as Euler characteristics of certain chain complexes in $\mathcal{R}_Q$.

</details>


### [90] [The index of subalgebras and strange coadjoint orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28796)
*Dmitri I. Panyushev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为代数群Q的子代数h的指数估计提供了新方法，利用余伴随Q-轨道理论，特别在稳定子与子代数互补时得到ind h=0的结论，并回答了Duflo问题。第二部分研究半单代数g中的“奇怪轨道”，证明了复杂度≤1的轨道、包含奇怪幂零轨道的片中的所有轨道都是奇怪的，并在sl_n中展示了奇怪轨道的丰富性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种通过余伴随Q-轨道来估计子代数h在q中指数的方法，并回答Duflo提出的问题。

Method: 利用余伴随Q-轨道的稳定子与子代数的互补关系进行指数估计；通过复杂度概念和片（sheet）理论对半单代数中的奇怪轨道进行分类刻画。

Result: 当q^ξ⊕h=q时，ind h=0；复杂度≤1的轨道是奇怪的；包含奇怪幂零轨道的片中的所有轨道都是奇怪的；sl_n中奇怪轨道并非稀疏。

Conclusion: 建立了子代数指数与余伴随轨道之间的新联系，深化了对半单代数中奇怪轨道的理解，并提出了若干猜想。

Abstract: For an algebraic group $Q$ with $\mathsf{Lie\,} Q=\mathfrak q$, we develop a method for estimating the index of a subalgebra $\mathfrak h$ in $\mathfrak q$ via the use of coadjoint $Q$-orbits in $\mathfrak q^*$. Let $\mathfrak q^ξ$ denote the stabiliser of $ξ\in\mathfrak q^*$. In the special case when $\mathfrak q^ξ\oplus\mathfrak h=\mathfrak q$, our estimate implies that $\mathsf{ind\,}\mathfrak h=0$. Using our theory, we also answer a question of Duflo. An orbit $Q{\cdot}η\subset\mathfrak q^*$ is said to be strange, if $\mathfrak q^η\oplus\mathfrak h=\mathfrak q$ for some $\mathfrak h$. In the second part of the paper, we study strange orbits for a semisimple algebra $\mathfrak g$. It is shown that an orbit ${\mathcal O}\subset\mathfrak g\simeq\mathfrak g^*$ is strange whenever the complexity of ${\mathcal O}$ is at most 1. Furthermore, if ${\mathcal S}\subset\mathfrak g$ is a sheet containing a strange nilpotent orbit, then all orbits in ${\mathcal S}$ are strange. We also show that strange orbits in $\mathfrak{sl}_n$ are not as sparse, as one might expect, and discuss some conjectures on strange orbits.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [A Structure-Preserving PML-Domain-Embedding Method for Acoustic Wave Scattering by Moving Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27680)
*Xuelong Gu,Qi Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种保结构的计算框架用于运动物体声波散射问题，包含新的PML-域嵌入模型和相容的数值逼近，无需重网格即可求解移动边界散射问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动物体声波散射问题求解需要不断重网格，计算效率低且复杂，亟需一种无需重网格的保结构计算方法。

Method: 将完美匹配层(PML)与域嵌入公式耦合，构建PML-DE系统；采用匹配渐近展开证明扩散界面公式收敛到尖锐界面系统；构造能量耗散率保持的有限差分格式；结合分层局部网格细化策略提高计算效率。

Result: 数值实验验证了散射解的准确性、吸收层和嵌入策略的有效性以及自适应算法的高效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为涉及复杂声波散射问题的工程应用提供了一种实用且鲁棒的计算方法。

Abstract: We develop a structure-preserving computational framework for acoustic wave scattering by moving objects, comprising a new PML-domain-embedding model and a compatible numerical approximation. The model couples a perfectly matched layer (PML), used to truncate the acoustic wave equation, with a domain-embedding formulation that represents moving objects on a fixed computational domain. The resulting PML-domain-embedding (PML-DE) system enables moving-boundary scattering problems to be solved without remeshing. Using matched asymptotic expansions, we show that the diffuse-interface formulation converges to the corresponding sharpinterface system as the interface thickness tends to zero. We then construct an energy-dissipationrate-preserving finite-difference scheme for the PML-DE system. To improve computational efficiency, the scheme is combined with hierarchical local refinement informed by the moving-object location, the fixed PML region, and the evolving wave dynamics, all within the fixed computational domain. Numerical experiments demonstrate the accuracy of the computed scattering solutions, the effectiveness of the absorbing layer and object-embedding strategy, and the efficiency of the adaptive algorithm. The proposed framework provides a practical and robust computational approach for engineering applications involving complex acoustic wave-scattering problems.

</details>

Conclusion: 该方法为准周期Helmholtz特征值问题提供了一种准确、鲁棒且可扩展的求解工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a spectral framework that embeds 1D and 2D quasiperiodic Helmholtz eigenvalue problems into higher-dimensional (2D and 4D) periodic spaces via the projection method \cite{jiang2014numerical, jiang2024numerical}. To effectively map the elevated high-dimensional states back to the original physical space, we establish a novel validation framework based on the weighted expectation of pointwise Rayleigh quotients. Supported by comprehensive error and spectral analysis, we demonstrate that the eigenvalues derived from this expectation align more authentically with the original quasiperiodic model, ultimately yielding a more appropriate and reliable eigenpair solution. Numerical experiments on continuous media demonstrate that our approach offers an accurate, robust, and scalable tool for solving quasiperiodic Helmholtz eigenvalue problems.

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在多种复杂几何域上均具有鲁棒性，基于FFT的扩散带来了显著的计算效率提升，并一致地生成高质量四边形网格。

Conclusion: 该工作为网格生成提供了可靠且理论完备的替代方案，在科学计算中具有广阔的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel, robust, and computationally efficient framework for high-quality quadrilateral mesh generation on general two-dimensional domains. The core of the proposed approach is a novel method for computing cross fields by minimizing a modified and relaxed Ginzburg--Landau-type energy functional. A key innovation is the extension of the problem from the original, potentially complex domain to a larger regular computational domain. This extension transforms the central computational procedure into an iterative scheme that requires only two straightforward and efficient operations: linear diffusion solved globally via the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and point-wise normalization. Notably, our method eliminates the conventional need for generating an intermediate triangular mesh or solving complex nonlinear optimization problems on the irregular domain. We provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, proving that the proposed iterative algorithm guarantees unconditional monotonic decay of the objective functional. Comprehensive numerical experiments demonstrate the method's robustness across a wide range of complex geometries, its significant computational efficiency afforded by the FFT-based diffusion, and its consistent generation of high-quality quadrilateral meshes. This work presents a reliable and theoretically sound alternative to existing mesh generation techniques, with strong potential for practical applications in scientific computing.

</details>

Abstract: The Virtual Element Method (VEM) is a well-established framework for solving partial differential equations on polygonal and polyhedral meshes. In this paper, we introduce a novel hybrid VEM that integrates both conforming and nonconforming virtual spaces. We apply this formulation to a three-dimensional linear elasticity problem, providing rigorous theoretical analysis to demonstrate optimal convergence rates. Furthermore, we explore the extension of this approach to domains with curved boundaries.

</details>


### [95] [Convergence analysis of a parareal algorithm with multistep fine propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28059)
*Georgios Akrivis,Qingle Lin,Zhi Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文针对抛物型方程，构建并分析了细传播器基于两步后向差分公式(BDF2)、粗传播器为单步法的并行时间积分算法(parareal)，提出了初始步校正方法并建立了线性收敛性，数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了相比已有方法的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用BDF2作为细传播器、单步法作为粗传播器构建parareal算法；设计了一种新颖的初始化步校正策略以处理多步法启动问题；通过理论分析证明迭代的线性收敛性。

Result: 数值结果充分支持理论发现，展示了相比现有基于多步法的parareal策略的明显改进，并表明该方法可有效扩展到高阶BDF方法及非线性问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的parareal算法在细传播器使用BDF2时仍保持线性收敛，所设计的校正策略有效，且方法具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: The parareal algorithm is a powerful parallel-in-time integration method that accelerates the numerical solution of evolution equations by iteratively combining a fine propagator and a coarse propagator. Although the convergence of the parareal algorithm has been extensively studied, most existing analyses assume that the fine propagator is either an exact solver or a single-step method. In this paper, we construct and analyze a parareal algorithm for solving parabolic equations, where the fine propagator is based on the two-step backward differentiation formula (BDF2), while the coarse propagator remains a single-step method. We propose a novel approach to design an effective correction for the initialization steps and establish linear convergence of the iteration. Numerical results fully support the theoretical findings, show clear improvements over existing multistep parareal strategies, and indicate that the proposed approach extends effectively to higher-order BDF methods and to nonlinear problems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Krylov子空间方法（如CG和BiCGSTAB）在求解大型Sylvester方程时存在计算效率和内存消耗大的问题，亟需一种能够利用方程低秩结构的高效求解框架。

Method: 重构面向矩阵的Krylov子空间方法，利用Sylvester算子的数学性质与右手边的低秩结构，将近似解表示为低秩因子分解形式，从而设计出数学等价但计算和存储更高效的算法。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提算法在求解大型Sylvester方程时具有高效的计算性能和较低的内存需求。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将Krylov子空间方法扩展到低秩格式下的Sylvester方程求解，在保证数学等价性的前提下显著提升了计算效率和内存可扩展性。

Abstract: Krylov subspace methods, such as the Conjugate Gradient (CG) and BiCGSTAB methods, are widely used in scientific computing for solving linear systems. In this study, we propose a new framework for solving large Sylvester equations in a low-rank format by reconstructing matrix-oriented Krylov subspace methods. The framework realizes efficient algorithms that are mathematically equivalent to the matrix-oriented Krylov subspace methods by exploiting the mathematical properties of the Sylvester operator and the low-rank structure of the right-hand side. Specifically, by leveraging these properties, approximate solutions can be expressed in a low-rank factorized form, enabling efficient computation and reduced memory requirements. The effectiveness of our algorithms is demonstrated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [97] [Preconditioned Discontinuous Galerkin Method for Elliptic Interface Problem on Unfitted Mesh with Reconstructed Discontinuous Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28380)
