  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆界面问题中，界面通常不拟合网格，导致标准有限元方法处理困难。现有重构间断逼近方法虽能构造任意高阶逼近空间，但求解大规模线性系统时缺乏高效预处理器。本文旨在设计一种条件数独立于网格尺寸、系数和界面位置的鲁棒预处理器，以提高求解效率。

Method: 提出改进的局部最小二乘重构方法：通过引入适当约束修改原始最小二乘问题，使重构空间在界面附近天然具有稳定性。建立高阶重构空间与分片常数空间之间的范数等价性，据此从分片常数空间构造预处理器。采用Nitsche惩罚法在切割间断Galerkin框架中弱强制界面跳跃条件，并设计多重网格算法求解最低阶系统矩阵的逆。

Result: 理论分析表明预处理系统的条件数上界与网格尺寸、系数跳跃大小及界面相对于非拟合网格的位置无关，具有最优性。二维和三维数值实验均证实了误差测度下的最优收敛速率，以及预处理方法的高效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于重构间断逼近的预处理非拟合有限元方法能有效解决椭圆界面问题，其预处理器具有网格、系数和界面位置无关的最优条件数，结合多重网格算法可大幅提升求解效率，为复杂界面问题的高效数值求解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an efficient preconditioned unfitted finite element method for the elliptic interface problem, based on the reconstructed discontinuous approximation. The approximation method for interface problems is originally proposed in [Li et al. SIAM J. Sci. Comput. 42(2), 2020], in which an arbitrarily high-order approximation space with one degree of freedom per element is constructed by solving local least squares fitting problems. The space can be applied within the cut discontinuous Galerkin framework, where the jump conditions across the interface are weakly enforced by the Nitsche's penalty method. In this work, the local least squares problem is modified by introducing appropriate constraints, which allows us to naturally ensure the stability near the interface by the reconstructed space, and further enables us to establish a norm equivalence between the high-order space and the lowest-order space. This equivalence property motivates us to construct a preconditioner from the piecewise constant space, and this preconditioning method is shown to be optimal in the sense that the upper bound of the condition number of the preconditioned system is independent of the mesh size, the coefficient and the interface location relative to the unfitted mesh. We also present the multigrid algorithms that serve as the inverse of the lowest-order system matrix. Numerical experiments in both two and three dimensions confirm the optimal convergence rates under error measurements and illustrate the efficiency and the robustness of the preconditioning method.

</details>


### [98] [Statistical comparison of reconstruction methods for the inverse boundary problem of the one-dimensional wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28415)
*Samuel Agenorwoth,Emilia Blåsten*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种一维波动方程逆边界值问题的数值重建算法：Sondhi-Gopinath (SG, 1971) 和 Korpela-Lassas-Oksanen (KLO, 2016)，基于1000个随机断面面积轮廓的统计测试发现，SG在低噪声条件下表现更优且实现简单、运行更快，而KLO在高噪声条件下更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前虽有研究指出SG和KLO两种算法均基于构造特定集合特征函数的解这一相似思想，但缺乏系统的性能对比。本文旨在填补这一空白，为实际应用中选择合适的重建算法提供依据。

Method: 生成n=1000个随机面积轮廓（不同平滑度水平），添加高达信号能量10%的测量噪声，对SG算法（1971年，已用于实际应用）和KLO算法（2016年，含理论证明的正则化方案，边界控制方法的演进）进行统计测试和性能比较。

Result: SG在低噪声条件下表现更优，KLO在高噪声条件下更具优势。SG更易实现且运行速度更快。两种算法标准边界数据相差一个时间导数，增加了分析复杂度。

Conclusion: 两种算法各有适用场景：低噪声环境推荐使用SG，高噪声环境推荐使用KLO。SG在工程实用性上（实现简易度和计算效率）具有明显优势。

Abstract: Several numerical reconstruction algorithms for the inverse boundary value problem of the 1-dimensional wave equation exist. In this paper we revisit two of them, the Sondhi-Gopinath (SG) method from 1971 and the Korpela-Lassas-Oksanen (KLO) method from 2016. The former is stable enough that it was used in practical applications. The latter has a regularisation scheme with a theoretical proof, and is an evolution of the boundary control method. Both are based on the idea of constructing solutions that are characteristic functions of a set at a given time. This similarity has been pointed out before, but no systematic comparison has been published.
  We compare the performance of the two algorithms with noisy simulated data. The application in our minds is reconstructing the internal cross-sectional area of a pressurised fluid pipe which corresponds to the first order $\partial_x$ term of the wave equation. Instead of just observing the performance on a few test cases, we generate $n=1000$ random area profiles of various smoothness levels and measurement noise up to $10\%$ of the signal energy and perform statistical tests. SG and KLO have a difference of one time-derivative in their standard boundary data, which complicates the analysis. Our results show that SG performs better in the low noise regime, and KLO with high noise. SG is easier to implement and runs faster.

</details>


### [99] [Physics-constrained identification of graph-based thermal networks for spacecraft digital twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28452)
*Luca Sosta,Carlo Ciancarelli,Leonardo Marini,Stefano Pagani,Francesco Regazzoni,Nicola Parolini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理约束的集总参数热模型（LPTM）校准框架，通过轨迹匹配从稀疏温度测量数据中重构航天器热动力学，无需先验参数值，并强制参数满足物理可行性（正系数与对称传导），在合成数据集上验证了模型对长期温度演变的准确预测能力及对噪声的鲁棒性，为航天器热数字孪生应用提供了高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、局部的温度测量数据中重建能够高效模拟航天器行为的热模型极具挑战性，现有方法依赖材料属性或几何假设的先验参数值，且缺乏系统性的物理约束校准框架。

Method: 将LPTM校准建模为图动力系统的轨迹逆问题，通过构造方式强制参数物理可行性（节点系数为正、传导对称），采用轨迹匹配进行参数辨识，无需额外正则化。

Result: 在多种复杂强迫条件下的高保真有限元合成数据集上，校准后的LPTM能够准确重现长期温度演化，对测量噪声具有鲁棒性，并在精度与计算效率之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为降阶热模型校准提供了一种系统化方法，结合物理结构与数据驱动辨识，适用于航天器热数字孪生集成。

Abstract: Reconstructing a thermal model capable of efficiently simulating the behavior of a spacecraft from sparse and localized temperature measurements remains a challenging task. To address this, we introduce a physically-constrained calibration framework for Lumped Parameter Thermal Models (LPTMs), formulated as a trajectory-based inverse problem for graph dynamical systems. The model reconstructs thermal dynamics directly from temperature measurements and known inputs, without relying on a priori parameter values derived from material properties or geometric assumptions. Physical admissibility is enforced at the parameterization level: positivity of nodal coefficients and symmetry of conductive interactions are imposed by construction. This guarantees stable dynamics and restricts the identification problem to a physically meaningful parameter space, improving conditioning without the need of additional regularization. The identification problem is addressed through trajectory matching, ensuring stable rollout over extended time horizons. The methodology is validated on synthetic datasets generated from high-fidelity finite element simulations under progressively complex forcing conditions. The calibrated LPTMs accurately reproduce long-term temperature evolution and exhibit robustness to measurement noise. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to the calibration of reduced-order thermal models by combining physical structure with data-driven identification. The numerical results show a favorable balance between accuracy and computational efficiency, making the models suitable for integration in spacecraft thermal Digital Twin applications.

</details>


### [100] [Wigner-Eckart Factorization of the Spectral Boltzmann Collision Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28475)
*René R. Hiemstra,Torsten Keßler,Michael R. A. Abdelmalik*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 通过将实验室坐标系刚性旋转对齐碰撞对并积分SO(3)群，将八维双线性玻尔兹曼碰撞算子的弱形式简化为五维运动学核心，在谱伽辽金框架下实现了精确的Wigner-Eckart分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低玻尔兹曼碰撞算子的计算维度与复杂度，解决传统稠密笛卡尔公式计算成本高、内存占用大的问题。

Method: 利用SO(3)旋转群对齐碰撞对，结合谱伽辽金框架（连带拉盖尔多项式与球谐函数）进行Wigner-Eckart分解；角几何由克菜布施-戈登系数精确计算，散射物理通过谱收敛奇异求积策略实现机器精度评估；显式置零宏观碰撞不变量嵌入。

Result: 单核加速比最高达37倍，内存减少1000倍；通过麦克斯韦分子解析解和硬球Chapman-Enskog粘度系数验证。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率与内存效率，为玻尔兹曼方程的高效数值求解提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We reduce the eight-dimensional weak form of the bilinear Boltzmann collision operator to a five-dimensional kinematic core by rigidly rotating the laboratory frame to align with the colliding pair and integrating over the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ rotation group. This reduction yields an exact Wigner--Eckart factorization within a spectral Galerkin framework of associated Laguerre polynomials and spherical harmonics. The decomposition decouples the angular geometry from the scattering physics. The former, represented by Clebsch--Gordan coefficients, is evaluated exactly, while the latter is evaluated to machine precision by a spectrally convergent singular quadrature strategy. By explicitly zeroing specific entries, the macroscopic collision invariants are embedded without approximation. Cache-optimized contractions deliver up to a 37-fold single-core speedup and a 1000-fold memory reduction over standard dense Cartesian formulations. The approach is validated against analytical solutions for Maxwell molecules and infinite-order Chapman--Enskog viscosity coefficients for hard spheres.

</details>

### [101] [Economic Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Microgrids with Generator Up and Downtime Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27553)
*Jürgen Gutekunst,Armin Nurkanovic,Ekaterina Kostina,Hans Georg Bock,Robert Scholz,Amer Mesanovic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对含离散决策变量（发电机运行时间与启动成本）的多阶段最优潮流问题，提出用凸二次近似替代非线性AC潮流方程，将大规模混合整数非线性规划简化为混合整数二次约束规划，并通过附加时间耦合约束保证递归可行性，基于周期耗散性假设证明稳定性，在6节点微电网仿真中验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经济非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）在多阶段最优潮流（OPF）中加入离散决策变量（如发电机运行时间、启动成本）后，导致大规模混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）计算困难的问题。

Method: 1) 用凸二次近似替代非线性AC潮流方程，将MINLP简化为MIQCP；2) 针对简单终端约束无法保证递归可行性的问题，提出附加时间耦合约束；3) 基于周期耗散性假设证明控制器稳定性。

Result: 所提方法使问题降为可被CPLEX等商用求解器高效求解的MIQCP，并通过时间耦合约束恢复了递归可行性，仿真实验在6节点微电网不同需求场景下验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 凸二次近似结合时间耦合约束是处理含离散决策变量的多阶段OPF问题的实用方案，在计算效率与理论保证之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Recently there has been a lot of progress in the development of economic nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) schemes for multistage optimal power flow (OPF) problems. However, the additional inclusion of discrete decision variables to model generator runtimes and generator startup costs can amount to large scale mixed-integer nonlinear programs (MINLPs) that are computationally very challenging. This work investigates the practical approach that replaces the nonlinear AC power flow equations by convex quadratic approximations. In combination with the discrete generator dynamics this leads to a mixed-integer quadratically constrained program (MIQCP) which is of significantly lower complexity and can be solved in reasonable time by off-the-shelf solvers such as CPLEX. We further show that simple terminal constraints are not sufficient to guarantee recursive feasibility of the NMPC scheme if constraints on generator runtime and on the number of generator startup events are present. To address this challenge we propose the use of additional time-coupled constraints and prove the resulting recursive feasibility property. Based on the assumption of periodic dissipativity of the underlying system we can prove stability of the proposed controller. To illustrate our results, we present simulations of a realistic 6-bus microgrid under different demand scenarios.

</details>


### [102] [Automated Synthesis of Lyapunov Functions for Multi-Agent Systems under Jointly Connected Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27576)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Lei Wang,Qing-Guo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统在联合连通拓扑下的一致性跟踪问题，通过自动合成多项式李雅普诺夫函数，提出分布式非线性控制协议，并利用平方和分解在多项式时间内实现一致性验证，同时将方法扩展到二阶系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联合连通拓扑下，传统的二次型李雅普诺夫函数可能不存在，需要更一般的多项式李雅普诺夫函数来实现一致性验证。

Method: 基于分布式非线性控制协议，通过平方和分解对多元多项式进行分解，自动合成多项式李雅普诺夫函数，在一阶和二阶多智能体系统中实现一致性验证。

Result: 建立了联合连通拓扑下一阶和二阶多智能体系统的一致性判据，可在多项式时间内有效验证一致性，并生成了多项式耦合函数。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效解决联合连通拓扑下的多智能体系统一致性问题，并通过算例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: This article investigates the consensus tracking problem of multi-agent systems under jointly connected topology through automated synthesis of Lyapunov functions. Based on the proposed distributed nonlinear control protocol, several consensus criteria for first-order multi-agent systems are established and certified by the construction and synthesis of more general polynomial Lyapunov functions. By employing sum-of-squares decomposition for multivariate polynomials, we can efficiently synthesize polynomial Lyapunov functions to achieve consensus verification in polynomial time, although the widely used quadratic Lyapunov functions do not exist. Moreover, polynomial coupling functions for our proposed protocol are concomitantly generated. Furthermore, the distributed nonlinear control protocol is extended to deal with second-order multi-agent systems, while ensuring second-order consensus verification. Finally, an example is presented to demonstrate the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [103] [OptiLoop: Coordination-in-the-Loop Verification and Repair for LLM-Generated Optimization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27630)
*Yujia Xu,Zhiheng Wang,Thi Dinh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出协调在环验证与修复方法（OptiLoop），用于LLM生成的优化智能体。通过ADMM共识协议进行短程协调运行，提取行为与静态证据驱动修复，在40个测试场景中将目标匹配率从66%提升至93%，社会匹配率从68.5%提升至89%，表明分散式决策中正确性验证应在协调环内而非孤立执行中进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分散式决策问题中，LLM生成的优化智能体可能出现语义错误（如成本误表示、约束范围错误、激励响应不正确），这些错误在孤立执行时无法被发现，只有在协调过程中才会以系统性行为失败的形式显现。因此需要在协调环内验证正确性。

Method: 提出OptiLoop流水线：从文本生成局部优化智能体，通过短程有界协调运行（基于ADMM共识协议）与固定参考对手方进行验证，提取结构化行为证据和静态证据，实施证据驱动的修复。当结构性而非实现性失败时，从局部代码修复升级为修正公式化重构，并可复用先前实例的经验。

Result: 在40个测试场景中，OptiLoop-Full将目标匹配率从66.0%提升至93.0%，社会匹配率从68.5%提升至89.0%，平均目标差距从15.3%降至3.5%，平均社会差距从7.6%降至2.0%。

Conclusion: 对于部署在分散式决策循环中的生成式优化智能体，正确性验证应在协调环内进行，而非仅通过孤立执行验证。

Abstract: Many decentralized decision problems require multiple parties to coordinate on shared decisions while keeping objectives, constraints, and data private. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising way to lower the barrier to participation by generating local optimization agents from natural-language specifications. In coordination settings, however, executability is not enough: a generated agent may compile, solve, and pass local checks while still being semantically wrong, for example by misrepresenting costs, mis-scoping constraints, or responding incorrectly to incentives. Such errors often surface only during coordination, as systematic behavioral failures rather than infeasibility. We propose coordination-in-the-loop verification and repair for LLM-generated optimization agents. We instantiate this idea with an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM)-style consensus protocol and introduce OptiLoop, a pipeline that generates local optimization agents from text, verifies them through short, bounded coordination runs against a fixed reference counterparty, extracts structured behavioral and static evidence, and applies evidence-driven repair. When failures are structural rather than implementational, OptiLoop escalates from localized code fixes to corrected-formulation repair, and it can additionally reuse episodic lessons from prior instances. On 40 held-out test scenarios, OptiLoop-Full improves objective match from 66.0% to 93.0% and social match from 68.5% to 89.0% relative to a strong local-validation baseline, while reducing mean objective gap from 15.3% to 3.5% and mean social gap from 7.6% to 2.0%. These results show that, for generated optimization agents deployed inside decentralized decision loops, correctness should be validated in the loop itself rather than through isolated execution alone.

</details>


### [104] [Polling Set Construction and Worst-Case Complexity for Direct Search under Polyhedral Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27814)
*Lindon Roberts,Clément W. Royer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为直接搜索方法（无导数优化）建立了新的理论基础，针对一般凸约束问题提出了复杂度保证。通过聚焦多面体凸约束，构建了包含近似切锥外方向的轮询集，理论证明了添加这些方向能提升性能，数值实验也验证了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接搜索方法和基于插值的方法在处理约束问题时存在理论差距。基于插值的方法已建立统一框架并具备复杂度保证，而直接搜索方法仍缺乏类似的理论基础。尤其对于约束问题，直接搜索缺乏理论依据来解释为何实际中在轮询集中包含切锥外方向能提升性能。

Method: 1) 为直接搜索方法引入新的理论框架，适用于一般凸约束并给出复杂度保证；2) 聚焦多面体凸约束，构建满足新理论要求的轮询集；3) 理论证明轮询集必须包含近似切锥外方向；4) 通过数值实验验证包含这些额外方向对实际性能的提升。

Result: 成功建立了直接搜索方法在凸约束下的新理论基础，提供了与模型方法相当的复杂度保证。证明了轮询集中包含切锥外方向的必要性，数值实验表明添加这些方向显著提升了实际性能。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了直接搜索与基于插值方法之间的理论差距，为约束直接搜索方法提供了统一的复杂度分析框架。同时为实践中添加切锥外方向的启发式策略提供了理论正当性。

Abstract: Direct search is one of the most popular derivative-free optimization paradigms, that relies on exploring the variable space using polling directions. To analyze and implement direct search, one typically relies on positive spanning sets. This concept is somewhat decorrelated from interpolation-based sets used in model-based algorithms, another class of derivative-free optimization methods. This discrepancy is even more pronounced for constrained problems, where recent advances in the interpolation-based setting have produced a unified picture that is still lacking in the direct-search case. In this paper, we introduce a new theoretical underpinning for direct-search methods, that can be defined for general convex constraints and lead to complexity guarantees, as in the model-based setting. By focusing on polyhedral convex constraints, we are able to construct polling sets that meet our new theoretical requirements. In particular, our polling sets necessarily include directions outside of the approximate tangent cone, giving theoretical justification to existing practical heuristics which incorporate this idea. Our numerical results confirm that adding these extra directions significantly improves practical performance.

</details>


### [105] [Optimal Spectral Design with Prior Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27837)
*Anton J. Kleywegt,Johannes Milz,Mohit Singh,Weijun Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类谱设计问题，其中先验半正定信息矩阵通过选定的设计向量（受欧几里得范数约束）构造的秩一矩阵之和进行更新，目标为更新后信息矩阵特征值的任意对称凸函数。该框架统一了A、D、E最优性等经典实验设计准则，并适用于基于模型的导数自由优化。通过特征值紧松弛获得凸重构，并利用Schur-Horn定理构造多项式时间算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一经典最优实验设计准则（A、D、E最优性），并解决导数自由优化中采样方向影响回归模型条件数与精度的问题。

Method: 使用特征值紧松弛将非凸问题转化为凸重构，应用Schur-Horn定理构造多项式时间算法。

Result: 算法可计算最优谱设计，数值实验展示了谱设计在导数自由优化中的潜力。

Conclusion: 谱设计问题可通过特征值松弛和Schur-Horn定理有效求解，并在导数自由优化中具有应用前景。

Abstract: We study a class of spectral design problems in which a prior positive semidefinite information matrix is updated by a sum of rank-one matrices constructed from chosen design vectors subject to a bound on their Euclidean norm. The objective of a spectral design problem is any symmetric convex function of the eigenvalues of the updated information matrix. This framework unifies classical optimal experimental design criteria, including A-, D-, and E-optimality. It also arises in model-based derivative-free optimization, where sampling directions determine the conditioning and accuracy of regression models. Although the objective is symmetric and convex in the eigenvalues, the optimization problem with design vectors/matrix as decision variables is nonconvex, and optimal solutions of their convex relaxations may not be feasible for the spectral design problem. We use tight eigenvalue relaxations to obtain a convex reformulation, and we apply the Schur--Horn theorem to construct a simple polynomial-time algorithm for solving the spectral design problem. We illustrate the optimal spectral designs computed by our algorithm. Moreover, a small set of numerical experiments shows the potential of spectral designs for derivative-free optimization.

</details>


### [106] [Stability of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27883)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了二次正则化最优输运（QOT）在边际、输运成本函数和正则化参数扰动下的定量稳定性理论，核心是L∞范数下对偶势的稳定性结果，并首次证明了二次成本下最优耦合支撑集的局部Lipschitz稳定性（关于Hausdorff距离）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOT作为熵正则化OT的稀疏替代方案，其稳定性性质尚未被充分理解，特别是最优耦合支撑集在扰动下的行为缺乏理论保证。

Method: 从随边际变化的L²空间误差界出发，利用势函数的自界性质推导出关于边际均匀的局部L∞-Lipschitz界，进而导出最优耦合及其支撑集的稳定性。

Result: 获得了对偶势在L∞范数下的稳定性结果；证明了二次成本下最优耦合支撑集在边际扰动下关于Hausdorff距离是局部Lipschitz稳定的。

Conclusion: 本文首次为正则化OT问题中最优支撑集提供了稳定性理论，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: Quadratically regularized optimal transport (QOT) is a sparse alternative to entropic optimal transport. We develop a quantitative stability theory for QOT under perturbations of the marginals, the transport cost function, and the regularization parameter. The centerpiece is an $L^\infty$-stability result for the dual potentials. Starting from an error bound in an $L^2$-space that varies with the marginals, we use a self-bound for the potentials to derive a local $L^\infty$-Lipschitz bound that is uniform over marginals. This bound also yields stability of the optimal coupling and of its support. In particular, we show that for the quadratic transport cost, the support of the optimal coupling is locally Lipschitz in Hausdorff distance under perturbations of the marginals. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first stability result for the optimal support in regularized optimal transport.

</details>


### [107] [Online Sketched Newton-Raphson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27985)
*Jean-Luc Lupien,Yuen-Man Pun,Youssef Diouane,Iman Shames,Antoine Lesage-Landry*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出在线草图牛顿-拉夫森方法(OSNR)，通过 sketching 技术降低二阶在线凸优化方法的计算复杂度（从立方级降至更低），同时保持紧致遗憾界，并在三个应用场景中实现亚线性动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶OCO方法虽能获得更紧致的遗憾界和更好的经验性能，但其矩阵求逆操作的计算复杂度为问题规模的立方级，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出OSNR方法，利用sketching（草图化/随机投影）技术近似二阶信息，避免昂贵的精确矩阵求逆，从而在保持理论优势的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Result: OSNR在在线求根、无约束OCO和时变等式约束OCO三个场景中均实现亚线性动态遗憾界；约束扩展版本OSNR-EC实现亚线性累积约束违反。

Conclusion: OSNR和OSNR-EC在数值实验（在线位置跟踪和最优潮流）中即使在低采样率下也表现出高性能，验证了理论优势与实用性的结合。

Abstract: In online convex optimization (OCO), a decision-maker is confronted with an unknown environment and seeks to play an optimal sequence of decisions on a short time-scale using only past information. Recent advances in second-order OCO methods have demonstrated tighter regret bounds and improved empirical performance over traditional first-order methods. However, this performance comes at a cost: a matrix inversion is now required, which scales with the cube of the size of the problem. In this work, we propose sketching to mitigate this limitation. Specifically, we present the online sketched Newton-Raphson method (OSNR) which preserves the tight regret bounds obtained with second-order methods while presenting a strict computational improvement in terms of complexity. We discuss three application scenarios of OSNR: online root finding, unconstrained OCO, and time-varying equality-constrained OCO, and present their respective regret and a constraint violation bound for the latter. In all three applications, OSNR achieves sublinear dynamic regret bounds. For the equality-constrained case, the extension OSNR with equality constraints OSNR-EC is shown to yield sublinear cumulative constraint violation. Finally, we illustrate the performance of OSNR and OSNR-EC on two numerical examples, viz., online position tracking and optimal power flow, and observe that OSNR and OSNR-EC exhibit high performance even at low sampling rates.

</details>


### [108] [Convergence of empirical subgradients for optimal transport-based objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28134)
*Tam Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于采样运输成本的参数化目标函数，证明了其次微分的图形收敛性，确保标准次梯度方法能一致收敛到总体问题的平稳点，并在风险规避优化、公平约束学习和切片Wasserstein问题中验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优运输损失在实践中常通过采样计算（如一维排序公式或切片Wasserstein成本），但其采样近似对优化稳定性的影响尚不明确。本文旨在建立采样运输成本目标函数次微分的收敛性理论，为这类方法在训练流程中的应用提供理论保证。

Method: 通过图形收敛（graphical convergence）理论，分析参数化目标函数在采样近似下次微分的收敛行为，证明其向总体目标次微分的收敛性，并分别在光滑和非光滑参数化设定下进行对比分析。

Result: 光滑参数化能够为统计一致性和优化稳定性提供良好的接口；而对于具有非光滑成本和模型的运输目标，其导数在大样本极限下可能表现出不稳定性。

Conclusion: 光滑参数化在基于采样的最优运输目标优化中至关重要，能保证次梯度方法的统计一致性和收敛稳定性；而非光滑设置可能导致优化不稳定，需要谨慎处理。

Abstract: Optimal transport is widely used to learn distributions, enforce distributional constraints, and model uncertainty. In applications, transport losses are often computed from samples through tractable representations, such as one-dimensional sorting formulas or sliced Wasserstein costs, making them practical components in training pipelines. We study parameterized objectives defined by sampled transport costs and prove graphical convergence of their subdifferentials to the subdifferential of the population objective. In particular, this ensures that standard subgradient methods consistently approach stationary points of the population-level problem. We illustrate the results in several settings, including risk-averse optimization, fairness-constrained learning, and sliced Wasserstein problems. Our analysis highlights that smooth parameterizations provide a favorable interface between statistical consistency and optimization. By contrast, transport objectives with nonsmooth costs and models may exhibit unstable derivatives in the large-sample limit.

</details>


### [109] [Dynamic Stabilisation of Boundary Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28189)
*Mohamed Fkirine,Lassi Paunonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Hilbert空间上的抽象线性边界控制系统，设计了基于观测器的控制器，提出了指数稳定、强稳定和多项式稳定的条件，并建立了闭环系统的外部适定性。通过一维波动方程、二维分布控制与观测波动方程及非均匀SCOLE模型验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对抽象线性边界控制系统，现有研究在基于观测器的控制器设计方面缺乏统一的稳定性理论框架，尤其缺乏涵盖指数、强和多项式稳定性的系统化条件及闭环系统外部适定性的严格分析。

Method: 采用算子半群理论与Lyapunov方法，针对Hilbert空间中的抽象线性边界控制系统设计基于观测器的动态输出反馈控制器，分别建立指数稳定、强稳定和多项式稳定的充分条件，并验证闭环系统的外部适定性。

Result: 给出了指数稳定、强稳定和多项式稳定的可验证条件；证明了闭环系统是外部适定的；在一维波动方程、二维分布控制与观测波动方程及非均匀SCOLE模型上成功实现了控制器设计。

Conclusion: 本文建立的基于观测器的控制框架能够有效稳定一大类抽象线性边界控制系统，统一处理不同类型的稳定性要求，所提出的条件具有实用性，数值例子验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We design observer-based controllers to stabilise abstract linear boundary control systems on Hilbert spaces. Our main results introduce conditions for exponential, strong, and polynomial stability, and establish external well-posedness of the closed-loop system. We design controllers for a one-dimensional wave equation, a two-dimensional wave equation with distributed control and observation, and a non-uniform SCOLE model.

</details>


### [110] [De-risking solutions to optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28240)
*Daniel Bienstock,Blake Sisson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种切割平面方法，用于调整优化问题的解，以减少资产或资源集中等风险暴露特征。该方法对风险表示形式不可知，旨在不显著增加名义成本的情况下快速降低风险指标，或证明无法实现这种调整。其底层方法借鉴了优化中一阶方法的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化方法往往只关注名义成本最小化，而忽略了资产或资源集中带来的风险暴露问题。需要一种方法能够在保持名义成本基本不变的前提下，有效降低风险指标。

Method: 提出一种切割平面方法，该方法对风险表示形式不可知（即不依赖于特定的风险度量方式），通过迭代调整优化问题的解来减少风险暴露特征。核心技术借鉴了优化领域的一阶方法（first-order methods）。

Result: 该方法能够快速降低适当的风险指标，而不显著增加名义成本；或者在无法实现调整时证明其不可行性。

Conclusion: 该切割平面方法论提供了一种高效、风险表示形式无关的途径来调整优化解，在控制成本的同时降低风险暴露，并具备快速收敛或不可行性证明的能力。

Abstract: We develop a cutting-plane methodology that adjusts solutions to optimization problems so
  as to reduce features that bring about exposure to risk, such as concentration of assets
  or resources. The methodology is agnostic to the representation of risk. Our procedure aims
  to reduce the appropriate risk metric without accruing a significant increase in nominal
  cost, rapidly, or proves that such an adjustment is not possible. The underlying approach
  borrows from techniques used in first-order methods for optimization.

</details>


### [111] [Hautus-Type Criteria for Controllability and Stabilizability of Backward-Structured Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28242)
*Jingrui Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对后向随机线性系统，提出了精确可控性和可镇定性的尖锐Hautus型判据，其核心发现是随机Hautus障碍并非确定性系统中的左特征向量或任意对称特征矩阵，而是Lyapunov型算子的正半定特征矩阵；精确可控性等价于不存在与控制方向正交的非零正半定特征矩阵，而可镇定性则由仅施加于非稳定谱部分的相同锥约束Hautus条件刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典确定性线性系统的Popov-Belevitch-Hautus检验推广到后向随机线性系统，解决其精确可控性与可镇定性的判别问题。

Method: 通过定义Lyapunov型算子，将随机Hautus障碍刻画为正半定特征矩阵（而非确定性系统的左特征向量），并引入锥约束（仅要求正半定特征矩阵满足正交性）和谱约束（仅针对非稳定谱部分）相结合的判据。

Result: 精确可控性等价于不存在非零正半定特征矩阵与控制方向正交；可镇定性由相同的锥约束Hautus条件仅施加于非稳定谱部分所刻画；此外还给出了有限秩、Gramian刻画、可控性分解以及精确可控性蕴含可镇定性的结论。

Conclusion: 随机Hautus理论由谱约束与锥约束共同支配，标志着随机系统可控性与确定性系统存在本质差异，且锥限制是尖锐的——排除所有对称特征矩阵是充分但不必要的。

Abstract: This paper develops sharp Hautus-type criteria, stochastic counterparts of the classical Popov-Belevitch-Hautus test, for exact controllability and stabilizability of backwardstructured stochastic linear systems. The main finding is that the stochastic Hautus obstruction is not a left eigenvector, as in deterministic linear systems, nor an arbitrary symmetric eigenmatrix, but a positive semidefinite eigenmatrix of a Lyapunov-type operator. We prove that exact controllability is equivalent to the absence of such nonzero positive semidefinite eigenmatrices that are orthogonal to the control directions. This cone restriction is sharp: excluding all symmetric eigenmatrices with the same orthogonality property is sufficient but not necessary. We further show that stabilizability is characterized by the same cone-restricted Hautus condition imposed only on the nonstable spectral part of the Lyapunov-type operator. Thus the stochastic Hautus theory developed here is governed by a simultaneous spectral restriction and cone restriction. In addition to these criteria, we provide finite-rank and Gramian characterizations underlying exact controllability, establish the corresponding controllability decomposition, and show that exact controllability implies stabilizability.

</details>


### [112] [Machine Learning for Exact Time Series Aggregation in Generation Expansion Planning with Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28356)
*Jakub Rybka,Luca Santosuosso,Thomas Klatzer,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习估计边际成本的迭代时间序列聚合（TSA）方法，用于解决含可再生能源、火电和储能技术的发电扩展规划（GEP）问题。该方法通过构建时间聚合模型，能够评估聚合模型与全规模模型的最优性间隙，并利用边际成本估计引导TSA保留原模型的关键约束，实现精确时间聚合。数值实验表明，该方法在聚合质量上显著优于传统仅依赖输入数据分析的TSA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEP模型计算负担大，传统TSA方法为纯启发式，无法评估聚合模型的最优性差距，且聚合质量有限。

Method: 提出迭代TSA方法，利用机器学习估计GEP模型边际成本，将其作为聚类特征引导时间聚合，保留原模型的活动约束，并能评估聚合模型与全规模模型的最优性间隙。

Result: 融合估计边际成本作为聚类特征显著提升了时间聚合质量，优于传统仅基于输入数据分析的TSA方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够实现精确时间聚合，有效缓解GEP计算负担，同时保证聚合模型的质量。

Abstract: This paper investigates a generation expansion planning (GEP) problem encompassing renewable, thermal, and storage technologies while simultaneously optimizing market participation, operational expenditures, and capital investment. To alleviate the computational burden of the GEP model, we propose a novel iterative time series aggregation (TSA) method that constructs a temporally aggregated counterpart of the original full-scale GEP model. Unlike traditional TSA methods, which are purely heuristic, our method enables the assessment of the optimality gap between the aggregated and full-scale models. Moreover, by leveraging machine learning-based estimates of the GEP model marginal costs, the algorithm guides TSA to construct an aggregated model that preserves the active constraints of its full-scale counterpart, which has been shown to yield exact temporal aggregation. Numerical results show that incorporating estimated marginal costs as clustering features substantially improves the quality of temporal aggregation compared with traditional TSA methods that rely solely on input data analysis.

</details>


### [113] [Operator-Norm Transfer and Cohomological Rigidity for Quaternionic Quasi-Lie Structures with Application to Sliding Mode $β$-Exponential Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28385)
*Nassim Athmouni,Nejib Brahmia,Tarek Fajraoui,Fehmi Mabrouk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对四元数拟Lie结构发展了算子理论与上同调工具，建立了算子范数转移、上同调刚性转录以及投影Jacobi缺陷的广义单边Lipschitz条件三个主要结果，并以此构建了一个基于积分滑模面的鲁棒控制框架，通过迭代LMI方案实现β-指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以滑模控制为应用驱动，为四元数拟Lie结构建立算子理论与上同调分析工具。

Method: 利用四元数反同构实现算子范数精确转移；将上同调刚性结果转录为局部非障碍假设下的双线性修正构造；通过均匀选择论证处理可测选择的状态依赖性，得到投影Jacobi缺陷的广义单边Lipschitz条件。

Result: 建立了三个主要定理：算子范数在左右模约定间无常数因子转移；上同调刚性下显式双线性修正Ω使括号在可容许半径球内精确满足Jacobi恒等式；投影Jacobi缺陷满足可计算常数的广义单边Lipschitz条件。

Conclusion: 构建了上同调匹配条件替代逐点验证的鲁棒控制框架，积分滑模面结合迭代LMI实现β-指数稳定性；工作局限于齐次拟Lie情形，Sobolev推广为猜想性质，算法终止依赖于LMI解映射的充分连续性假设。

Abstract: We develop operator-theoretic and cohomological tools for quaternionic quasi-Lie structures, with sliding mode control as a motivating application. Three main results are established. First, an exact operator-norm transfer under the quaternionic anti-isomorphism $a \mapsto \bar{a}$, which enables quantitative bounds from~\cite{Athmouni2026} to transfer between left- and right-module conventions with no constant factor. Second, a transcription of the cohomological rigidity result of~\cite{Athmouni2026} into a form usable here: in the homogeneous case, under a local cohomological non-obstruction hypothesis, an explicit bilinear correction $Ω$ produces a bracket satisfying the Jacobi identity exactly on a ball of admissible radius, with all quantitative constants expressed through $C_{2}$ and the admissible radius. Third, the projected Jacobi defect is shown to satisfy a generalized one-sided Lipschitz condition with computable constants, obtained via a uniform-selection argument handling state-dependence of the measurable selection. As an application, we develop a robust control framework with a cohomological matching condition replacing pointwise verification: an integral sliding surface yields $β$-exponential stability via an iterative linear matrix inequality (LMI) scheme. The work is purely analytical; closed-loop numerical simulations for multidimensional systems are deferred to a companion paper. The framework is restricted to the homogeneous quasi-Lie case; the Sobolev extension is conjectural, and algorithm termination is established conditionally on sufficient continuity assumptions on the LMI solution map.

</details>


### [114] [Birkhoff interpolation models for optimization with some available derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28503)
*Jeffrey Larson,Matt Menickelly,Evan Toler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Birkhoff插值的无导数优化框架，能够在仅有部分导数可用（如伴随仿真、遗留代码等场景）时，利用函数值和混合偏导数信息构建局部多项式模型，并集成到信赖域方法中，实现从无导数到有导数优化的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际科学计算中，许多场景下只能获得部分导数信息（如伴随仿真、遗留软件、部分可微模型），而传统的无导数优化或基于导数的方法均无法有效利用这种混合信息。现有的Hermite插值要求所有可用导数在同一插值点查询，过于严苛。

Method: 1) 引入Birkhoff插值框架，允许任意模式下的导数可用性，混合使用函数值和偏导数信息构建局部多项式模型；2) 提出插值系统适定性条件并建立完全二次插值模型的精度界；3) 开发信赖域框架，在保持插值集适定性的同时选择性纳入导数信息；4) 该框架自然推广了经典的无导数优化信赖域算法。

Result: 在CUTEst测试问题上使用合成的导数可用性模式进行评估，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法统一了无导数和有导数优化的边界，为混合导数可用性场景提供了一种理论和实践兼顾的优化框架。

Abstract: We consider interpolation-based derivative-free optimization in settings where only some derivatives are available. Such situations arise naturally in scientific computing applications involving simulations, adjoint-enabled components, legacy software, or partially differentiable models. We introduce a Birkhoff interpolation framework that permits arbitrary patterns of derivative availability and enables the con- struction of local polynomial models using mixtures of function values and partial derivative informa- tion. In contrast to Hermite interpolation approaches, the proposed framework does not require all available derivatives to be queried at every interpolation point. We develop conditions under which the resulting interpolation systems are poised and establish corresponding model-accuracy bounds for fully quadratic interpolation models. We develop a trust-region framework that maintains poised interpolation sets while selectively incorporating derivative information. The method generalizes an established class of interpolation-based derivative-free optimization algorithms and naturally bridges derivative-free and derivative-based settings. We evaluate the proposed approach on a collection of CUTEst test problems with synthetically generated derivative-availability patterns.

</details>


### [115] [Model Predictive Control for Constrained Linear Positive Systems on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28550)
*Roland Schurig,David Ohlin,Anders Rantzer,Emma Tegling,Rolf Findeisen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有非负状态和输入的正线性系统（如路由、物流和隔室模型），在状态（存储）和输入（节点间流量）存在容量约束的情况下，利用无约束问题的解析结构构建了显式次优容许控制器，得到了图可计算的性能界和模型预测控制器的最小稳定化时域长度，并通过凸规划高效计算最优界和时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正线性系统广泛存在于路由、物流和隔室建模等实际应用中，添加容量约束后问题复杂度显著增加，需要利用系统结构设计可计算的控制保证。

Method: 基于无约束问题的解析结构，构建显式次优容许控制器；推导图可计算的性能界和MPC最小稳定化时域长度；通过凸规划计算最优界和时域。

Result: 得到了图可计算的性能界和最小稳定化时域长度；凸规划可高效计算最优界和时域。

Conclusion: 系统结构使得显式MPC保证得以实现，而这在一般情况下是难以获得的。

Abstract: Positive systems describing networks with inherently non-negative states and inputs arise naturally in routing, logistics, and compartmental modelling. We consider problems modelled as positive linear systems in incidence form with linear cost. The addition of capacity constraints on states (storage) and inputs (flows between nodes) significantly increases the problem complexity. Leveraging the analytic structure of the unconstrained problem, an explicit suboptimal admissible controller is constructed. This yields graph-computable performance bounds and a minimum stabilising horizon length for a model predictive controller without terminal conditions. A convex program enables efficient computation of the optimal bound and horizon. These results highlight how system structure enables explicit MPC guarantees that are typically not available.

</details>


### [116] [Implicit Regularization in Perturbed Deep Matrix Factorization: Spectral Conditions and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28613)
*Jingzhe Wang,Hung-Hsu Chou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声干扰下深度矩阵分解中低秩隐式正则化的稳定性，推导了梯度下降在无噪声情况下出现低秩阶段的谱条件，分析了扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响，并证明了低秩阶段在扰动下仍能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度矩阵分解中低秩隐式正则化在面对噪声干扰时的稳定性，即当目标矩阵被噪声破坏时，梯度下降是否仍能表现出低秩偏好。

Method: 首先推导无噪声场景下梯度下降出现低秩阶段的充分谱条件，建立目标谱、初始化和步长与低秩区间存在性的关系；然后分析扰动梯度下降动力学，给出收敛性保证并量化扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响。

Result: 导出了无噪声情况下低秩阶段存在性的充分谱条件；证明了扰动下梯度下降的收敛性；量化了扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响；表明低秩阶段在扰动下仍能保持，且显式依赖于扰动大小。

Conclusion: 低秩隐式正则化在噪声干扰下具有稳定性，扰动大小与低秩阶段的保持之间存在显式依赖关系，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: This paper studies the stability of low-rank implicit regularization in perturbed deep matrix factorization, where the target matrix is corrupted by a noise matrix. We first derive sufficient spectral conditions under which gradient descent exhibits a low-rank phase in the noiseless setting. These conditions show how the target spectrum, initialization, and step size jointly determine the existence of a nonempty low-rank interval. We then analyze the perturbed gradient descent dynamics, proving convergence guarantees and quantifying how the perturbation affects iteration complexity and eigenvalue recovery. Finally, we show that the low-rank phase persists under perturbation, with explicit dependence on the perturbation size. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [117] [Disjunctive Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28674)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Sanjeeb Dash,Yixuan Hua,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出析取平方和（DSOS）方法，通过多个低次代数恒等式并行验证多项式非负性，并构建了基于半定规划的收敛下界层级，其中最大半定约束的规模在整个层级中固定。此外还提出了无优化层级，并将其应用于矩阵协正性的判定，最后结合分支定界算法进行了数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平方和（SOS）方法用一个代数恒等式证明多项式非负性，但当多项式次数较高时，SOS所需的半定规划规模会迅速增大，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过多个低次恒等式的析取组合来降低单个半定约束的规模，使计算更加可行。

Method: 1. 提出析取平方和（DSOS）概念，用多个低次代数恒等式的析取来证明非负性；2. 证明析取Positivstellensatz，保证每个恒等式的次数可控制在原多项式次数以内；3. 构造固定最大半定约束规模的SDP收敛层级；4. 提出无需优化的第二析取Positivstellensatz层级，并特化到矩阵协正性问题；5. 与分支定界算法结合进行数值实验。

Result: 证明了析取Positivstellensatz，建立了具有固定半定规模的可收敛下界层级；提出了无优化层级；在多项式优化、协正优化和组合优化问题上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 析取平方和方法为多项式非负性验证提供了一种新的并行化框架，能够在保持较低单次计算成本的同时获得收敛的全局下界，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of disjunctive sum of squares for certifying nonnegativity of polynomials. Unlike the popular sum of squares approach where nonnegativity is certified by a single algebraic identity, the disjunctive sum of squares approach certifies nonnegativity with multiple algebraic identities which can be found in parallel. Our main result is a disjunctive Positivstellensatz proving that we can keep the degree of each algebraic identity as low as the degree of the polynomial whose nonnegativity is in question. Based on this result, we construct a semidefinite programming based converging hierarchy of lower bounds for the problem of minimizing a polynomial over a compact basic semialgebraic set, where the size of the largest semidefinite constraint is fixed throughout the hierarchy. We further prove a second disjunctive Positivstellensatz which leads to an optimization-free hierarchy for polynomial optimization. We specialize this result to the problem of proving copositivity of matrices. Finally, we describe how the disjunctive sum of squares approach can be combined with a branch-and-bound algorithm and we present numerical experiments on polynomial, copositive, and combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [118] [Adaptive Partitioning in Column Generation for Nested Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28692)
*Bart van Rossum,Rolf van Lieshout,Alexandre Jacquillat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对嵌套路径问题提出了一种新的列生成精确定价算法，通过自适应桶划分和迭代定价步骤，在避免枚举非支配子路径的同时保持精确性，在两个应用场景中取得了显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套路径问题中，路径变量可分解为子路径序列，子路径需满足局部资源约束，路径还需满足全局资源约束。现有方法需要枚举非支配子路径，计算效率低下，亟需更高效的精确定价算法。

Method: 提出基于自适应桶划分的列生成定价算法：将子路径按全局资源消耗划分为桶，每个桶仅保留最小化缩减成本的子路径；通过悲观和乐观定价步骤交替进行，组合子路径为完整路径并维护缩减成本上下界；采用自适应细化过程在有限次迭代内闭合上下界间隙。

Result: 在平衡多周期容量车辆路径问题中，相比最先进的列生成基准方法实现最高13倍加速，分支定价切割算法解决的实例数达到子路径基线的3倍；在鲁棒铁路乘务调度问题中实现最高3倍加速，原始解与最优解偏差在1%以内。

Conclusion: 所提算法在避免枚举非支配子路径的同时保持了精确性，显著提升了嵌套路径问题的求解效率，在车辆路径和铁路调度两个具有挑战性的应用上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: We study a class of nested path problems, in which every path-based variable can be decomposed into a sequence of subpaths. Subpaths must satisfy local resources, while paths must satisfy additional global resources. This paper develops a new exact pricing algorithm in column generation for these problems that avoids the enumeration of non-dominated subpaths. The algorithm relies on adaptive partitioning of subpaths into buckets characterizing the consumption of global path resources. The algorithm represents each bucket by its subpath of minimum reduced cost, and iterates between pessimistic and optimistic pricing steps to combine subpaths into paths while maintaining upper and lower bounds on the minimum reduced cost. An adaptive refinement procedure closes the gap in a finite number of iterations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm on two applications. For the balanced multi-period capacitated vehicle routing problem, we obtain speed-ups of up to a factor of 13 over a state-of-the-art column generation benchmark, and the resulting branch-price-and-cut algorithm solves three times as many instances to optimality as a subpath-based baseline. For the robust railway crew scheduling problem, we obtain speed-ups of up to a factor of three and produce primal solutions within 1% of optimality.

</details>


### [119] [Robust Markov Decision Processes on Continuous State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28706)
*Mengmeng Li,Yifan Hu,Daniel Kuhn,Yan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续状态空间上的无限时域鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程，提出了结构化矩形模糊集，并基于动态规划形式引入随机一阶方法进行鲁棒策略评估与优化，分别实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$的样本复杂度，该结果为连续状态空间下的新结果，并可直接推广至零和马尔可夫博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒MDP研究多集中于离散状态空间或有限状态设置，连续状态空间下的样本复杂度分析尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一理论空缺，提供连续状态空间下鲁棒策略评估与优化的高效算法及其样本复杂度保证。

Method: 采用结构化矩形模糊集（位于未知生成核的凸包内），利用鲁棒MDP的动态规划形式，提出随机一阶方法进行鲁棒策略评估，并在此基础上结合近似策略迭代方法求解$\varepsilon$-最优策略。

Result: 鲁棒策略评估与策略优化均达到$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$的样本复杂度，且该复杂度对连续状态空间而言是首次获得。此外，该方法可直接应用于零和马尔可夫博弈，并严格改进了现有连续状态空间的样本复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 本文为连续状态空间下的无限时域鲁棒MDP提供了首个具有$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$样本复杂度的算法，同时方法具有通用性，可推广至零和博弈场景。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon robust Markov decision processes (MDPs) on continuous state spaces with structured rectangular ambiguity set. The proposed ambiguity set falls within the convex hull of unknown generating kernels. We utilize the dynamic formulation of the corresponding robust MDPs, and subsequently introduce a stochastic first-order method for robust policy evaluation. We establish its high probability convergence to the robust value function, which in turn leads to an $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$ sample complexity. This high probability accuracy certificate is then used in an approximate policy iteration method that finds an $ε$-optimal policy with $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$ samples. The obtained sample complexities for both robust policy evaluation and optimization appear to be new for robust MDPs with continuous state spaces. Of independent interest, the proposed method is also directly applicable to zero-sum Markov games, which seems to strictly improve the existing sample complexities for continuous state spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Some Remarks on $τ$-Congruent Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27639)
*Shamik Das,Debajyoti De*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Chahal等人的工作，研究了内切于单位圆和外切于单位椭圆的Heron三角形及有理直角三角形，并探讨了由这些三角形生成的τ-同余数、同余数以及旁切圆相关的同余数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前人关于三角形与同余数研究的基础上，进一步探索更一般的几何构造（如紧贴单位圆/椭圆的三角形、旁切圆）与数论中同余数概念之间的联系。

Method: 通过将几何条件（三角形紧贴单位圆、单位椭圆、旁切圆）转化为代数方程，利用数论方法刻画满足条件的三角形边长特征，并从中提取相应的同余数。

Result: 1. 确定了所有紧贴单位圆的Heron三角形及其生成的τ-同余数；2. 刻画了所有紧贴单位椭圆的有理直角三角形及其对应的同余数；3. 研究了有理直角三角形旁切圆相关的同余数。

Conclusion: 本文系统地扩展了三角形几何与同余数理论之间的联系，为后续研究提供了新的分类结果和构造方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the work of \cite{Chahal} in several directions. We first determine all Heron triangles that tightly circumscribe the unit circle and the associated $τ$-congruent numbers generated by them. We then characterize all rational right triangles that tightly circumscribe the unit ellipse and identify the corresponding congruent numbers. In addition, we study of the congruent numbers from the excircle opposite a vertex of a rational right triangle, that is, the circle tangent to one side of the triangle and to the extensions of the remaining two sides.

</details>


### [121] [Linnik's problem for multiplicative functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27833)
*Kaisa Matomäki,Joni Teräväinen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究乘法函数符号变化在算术级数中的最小出现位置，证明除实Dirichlet特征伪装这一自然障碍外，在每个既约剩余类中都能在不超过q^{2+o(1)}范围内找到符号相反的平方自由整数，这改进了Liouville函数的现有结果并达到此前仅在GRH下成立的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Linnik问题（算术级数中最小素数）推广到乘法函数的符号变化场景，探究乘法函数符号在剩余类中出现的下界。

Method: 利用解析数论方法，处理乘法函数的符号分布，排除实Dirichlet特征伪装的退化情形，通过精细的指数和估计与筛法技术得到上界。

Result: 证明对于任意乘法函数h和非平凡剩余类a mod q，存在平方自由整数n1,n2≡a (mod q)且h(n1)<0<h(n2)，满足n1,n2 ≤ q^{2+o(1)}，除非h的符号强烈伪装成模q的实Dirichlet特征。

Conclusion: 该结果本质上达到了平方根障碍，对Liouville函数改进了Ford和Radziwiłł的近期结果，并匹配了此前在广义Riemann假设下才成立的最优界。

Abstract: We study a multiplicative function analogue of Linnik's problem on the least prime in an arithmetic progression. Let $h\colon \mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{R}\setminus\{0\}$ be a multiplicative function, and let $a \pmod q$ be a reduced residue class. We ask how far one must go before finding square-free integers $n_1,n_2\equiv a \pmod q$ with $h(n_1)<0<h(n_2)$. We show that one can always find such integers with $n_1,n_2\le q^{2+o(1)}$, unless the sign of $h$ strongly pretends to be a real Dirichlet character modulo $q$. Thus, apart from this natural character obstruction, sign changes of a multiplicative function occur in every reduced residue class at a scale corresponding essentially to the square root barrier. In the special case of the Liouville function $λ$ this improves on a recent result of Ford and Radziwiłł and matches, up to $q^{o(1)}$ factors, what was previously known conditionally under the generalized Riemann hypothesis.

</details>


### [122] [$Q$-difference analogue of the Stothers-Mason theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27876)
*Jian-Tang Lu,Xing-Xing Lu,Zhi-Tao Wen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了零点q-权重的新定义（当q→1时退化为零点重数），并通过新的q-差分根概念得到了q-差分版本的Stothers-Mason定理（当q→1时退化为经典Stothers-Mason定理），进而应用于研究q-差分Fermat型函数方程的多项式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的复分析中零点重数和Stothers-Mason定理推广到q-差分领域，建立q-差分版本的代数独立性理论框架。

Method: 引入零点q-权重的新定义，定义q-差分根，通过q-差分版本的算子推导得到对应的Stothers-Mason定理，并应用于Fermat型q-差分方程。

Result: 得到了q-差分版本的Stothers-Mason定理，该定理在q→1时退化为经典情形；并利用该定理研究了q-差分Fermat型方程的多项式解。

Conclusion: 成功将零点重数和Stothers-Mason定理推广到q-差分情形，为q-差分函数方程的可解性提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a new definition of the $q$-weight of zeros, which reduces to the multiplicity of zeros as $q\to 1$. Furthermore, we obtain a $q$-difference version of the Stothers-Mason theorem by means of the new definition of the $q$-difference radical, which covers the classical Stothers-Mason theorem as $q\to 1$. As applications, we study the polynomial solutions of $q$-difference Fermat type functional equations.

</details>


### [123] [Good Integers: (T,k)-Subclasses and Applications to Galois Duality in Coding Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27933)
*Somphong Jitman,Panthakan Boonsuriyatham*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了一类新的"好整数"——关于序列 (a^{ks+T}+b^{ks+T})_{s≥1} 的 (T,k)-好整数，建立了其算术理论（包括奇素数幂刻画、奇整数的2-adic赋值刻画、偶整数的处理及判定算法），并将其应用于编码理论，给出了 q^k-分圆类对 Galois 自互反不可约因子、Galois LCD 循环码和 Galois 自对偶循环码的特征刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的好整数（即序列 (a^s+b^s) 的因子）在 Abel 码和循环码的 Euclidean 与 Hermitian 对偶性研究中已成为重要的算术工具，本文旨在推广这一概念至更一般的序列，从而拓展其在编码理论中的应用。

Method: 定义了(T,k)-好整数，建立了奇素数幂刻画的算术理论；利用2-adic赋值给出了奇整数的通用刻画；对偶整数进行了分类处理；设计了判定整数是否为(T,k)-好整数并计算对应指数s的显式算法；将结果特例化为(a,b)=(q,1)，利用q^k-分圆类刻画Galois对偶性。

Result: 给出了(T,k)-好整数在奇素数幂、一般奇数和偶数情况下的完整刻画；提出了一个判定与计算的显式算法；在编码理论中，q^k-分圆类刻画了Galois自互反不可约因子，给出了Galois LCD循环码的描述与计数，以及Galois自对偶循环码的特征刻画。

Conclusion: 本文建立的(T,k)-好整数理论推广了经典好整数概念，为循环码的Galois对偶性研究提供了统一的算术框架，具有理论意义和编码应用价值。

Abstract: The notion of good integers, namely the divisors of the sequence $(a^s+b^s)_{s\ge 1}$ for nonzero coprime integers $a$ and $b$, together with their subfamilies such as oddly-good and evenly-good integers, has become an important arithmetic tool in the study of Euclidean and Hermitian dualities for abelian and cyclic codes. Building on this perspective, this paper introduces and studies another interesting subclass of good integers arising from the sequence $\bigl(a^{ks+T}+b^{ks+T}\bigr)_{s\ge 1}$ for some integers $0\leq T<k$, whose divisors are called $(T,k)$-{\em good integers with respect to} $(a,b)$. An arithmetic theory of these integers is developed, including a characterization at odd prime powers, a general characterization for odd integers in terms of $2$-adic valuations, and a treatment of even integers. An explicit algorithm is also given for deciding whether a given integer $d$ is $(T,k)$-good with respect to $(a,b)$ and, when it is, for computing an exponent $s$ such that $d\mid \bigl(a^{ks+T}+b^{ks+T}\bigr)$. Applications in coding theory are then obtained from the specialization $(a,b)=(q,1)$, where $q$ is a prime power. In particular, the $q^k$-cyclotomic classes of the cyclic group $\mathbb Z_n$ characterize the Galois self-reciprocal irreducible factors of $x^n-1$ over $\F_{q^k}$, give a description and enumeration of Galois LCD cyclic codes of length $n$ over $\F_{q^k}$, and lead to a characterization of Galois self-dual cyclic codes.

</details>


### [124] [Mixed thresholds in the Lonely Runner Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27941)
*Alathea Jensen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究孤独跑者猜想的变体，允许不同跑者有不同的距离阈值，精确刻画了二跑者情况下的可达距离向量集，并利用傅里叶级数得到算术级数求和公式和双函数积分公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典孤独跑者猜想要求所有跑者达到相同的距离下界(1/(k+1))，但实际问题中不同跑者可能需要不同的安全距离。因此，研究允许距离阈值随跑者变化的情况更具一般性和实用性。

Method: 定义MLPS_k为所有可能的距离阈值向量集合，使得对任意不同正整数速度，存在时间t使各跑者距离≥对应阈值。对k=2给出精确刻画；利用傅里叶级数构造距离阈值指示函数，推导算术级数求和公式和双函数积分公式。

Result: 完全刻画了二跑者情形下的MLPS_2集合；得到了关于不等阈值的算术级数求和公式和精确的双函数积分公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为不等阈值的孤独跑者问题提供了理论工具，二跑者情形的精确刻画和傅里叶分析方法为后续高维情形研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The Lonely Runner Conjecture states that if $k+1$ runners start at the same point on a unit-length circular track and run with distinct constant speeds, then each runner is at some time at least $1/(k+1)$-distant from every other runner. Equivalently, for every tuple of $k$ distinct positive integer speeds $s_1,\ldots,s_k$, there is a real number $t$ such that $\|s_i t\|\geq \frac{1}{k+1}$ for all $i$.
  We introduce and study a version of the conjecture in which the required distances may vary with $i$. For $\mathbf d=(d_1,\ldots,d_k)\in(0,1/2]^k$, let $\mathsf{MLPS}_k$ be the set of vectors such that, for every choice of distinct positive integer speeds $s_1,\ldots,s_k$, there is a real number $t$ with $\|s_i t\|\geq d_i$ for all $i$.
  We give an exact characterization of $\mathsf{MLPS}_2$. We also use Fourier series for distance-threshold indicator functions to obtain an arithmetic progression summation formula and an exact two-function integral formula for unequal thresholds.

</details>


### [125] [Transformation Formulae and Applications for Double Lambert Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28393)
*Rong Chen,Tianjian Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类双重Lambert级数，建立了恒等式和变换关系，可将双重级数简化为单重级数，并应用于证明多个近期猜想及给出已有结果的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重Lambert级数在数论和q-级数领域有重要应用，但目前缺乏系统性的化简工具。作者旨在建立一般性变换公式，以便将复杂的双重级数约化为更易处理的单重级数。

Method: 通过解析推导和代数操作，建立双重Lambert级数的恒等式与变换关系，核心是将双重求和转化为单重求和。研究过程中利用了已知的Lambert级数性质及q-级数技巧。

Result: 获得了若干关键恒等式和变换公式，成功将双重Lambert级数约化为单重Lambert级数。应用这些公式证明了Andrews-Dixit-Schultz-Yee猜想和Amdeberhan-Andrews-Ballantine猜想，并给出了后一结果的新证明。

Conclusion: 所建立的变换关系是处理双重Lambert级数的有效工具，验证了多个公开猜想的正确性，并为相关领域的进一步研究提供了方法论支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of double Lambert series and establish several identities and transformation relations for them. These formulae provide useful tools for reducing certain double Lambert series to single Lambert series. As applications, we derive identities related to recent conjectures of Andrews, Dixit, Schultz, and Yee, and of Amdeberhan, Andrews, and Ballantine. We also propose a new proof of a result of Amdeberhan, Andrews, and Ballantine.

</details>


### [126] [Additive Diophantine Equations involving S-Units, Factorials and Ternary Recurrences with repeated root](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28449)
*Vikas Godara,Divyum Sharma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于固定整数k和有界整数a_1,...,a_k，Cullen数C_n减去a_1m_1!-...-a_km_k!的最大素因子有效趋向无穷，并进一步推广到三元递归序列族；同时求解了C_n = m_1! + m_2! + s（s的素因子仅为2,3,5,7）的丢番图方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，寻找具有特定丢番图性质的Cullen数引起了研究兴趣。作者旨在研究Cullen数与阶乘组合的算术性质，特别是最大素因子的增长性，并求解相关丢番图方程。

Method: 运用解析数论和丢番图逼近的方法，通过有效估计证明最大素因子趋于无穷；采用初等数论和枚举方法求解特定形式的丢番图方程。

Result: 1) 固定整数k和有界整数a_i时，C_n - Σa_i m_i!的最大素因子有效趋向无穷。2) 该结论推广到更一般的三元递归序列族。3) 完全求解了C_n = m_1! + m_2! + s（s的素因子仅含2,3,5,7）的丢番图方程。

Conclusion: Cullen数与阶乘组合的最大素因子具有增长性，且特定形式的丢番图方程可解。

Abstract: Let $C_n=n2^n+1$ denote the $n$th Cullen number. There has been recent interest in finding all Cullen numbers having a given Diophantine property. We prove that, for a fixed integer $k$ and bounded integers $a_1,\ldots,a_k$, the greatest prime divisor of $C_n-a_1m_1!-\cdots-a_km_k!$ tends to infinity, in an effective way. We prove this for some more general families of ternary recurrence sequences as well. We also solve the Diophantine equation $$C_n = m_1! + m_2! + s,$$ where $s$ is a positive integer composed of primes $2,3,5,7$.

</details>


### [127] [Metrical theory of signed Engel expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28530)
*Can Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符号Engel展开，将(0,1)中的无理数唯一表示为交错符号的级数形式，并研究了数字序列的极限定理与Borel-Bernstein型零一律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受经典Engel展开和Pierce展开的启发，作者希望引入一种带有正负符号的Engel型展开，以拓展连分数理论中符号展开的框架，并研究其数论与度量性质。

Method: 定义了一种符号Engel展开，其中ε₁(x)=1，εₙ(x)∈{1,-1}（n≥2），数字dₙ(x)满足当εₙ₊₁(x)=-εₙ(x)时dₙ₊₁(x)≥dₙ(x)+2，且dₙ(x)为非递减的偶正整序列并趋于无穷。利用概率论与度量数论工具分析数字序列的统计行为。

Result: 得到了数字dₙ(x)及其差分Δₙ(x)=dₙ(x)-dₙ₋₁(x)的强大数律、中心极限定理和重对数律；建立了关于比值Rₙ(x)=dₙ(x)/dₙ₋₁(x)的Borel-Bernstein型零一律，刻画了集合{x∈(0,1): Rₙ(x)≥φ(n) i.o.}的Lebesgue测度。

Conclusion: 符号Engel展开具有良好的度量性质，其数字序列满足经典的概率极限定理，且比值增长率的临界条件可由Borel-Bernstein定理精确刻画。

Abstract: Motivated by the Engel and Pierce expansions, we introduce a signed Engel expansion. We expand each $x\in(0,1)\setminus\mathbb{Q}$ uniquely as
  $$x=\frac{ε_{1}(x)}{d_{1}(x)}+\frac{ε_{2}(x)}{d_{1}(x)d_{2}(x)}+\cdots+\frac{ε_{n}(x)}{d_{1}(x)d_{2}(x)\cdots d_{n}(x)}+\cdots,$$
  where $ε_{1}(x)\coloneqq1$ and $ε_{n}(x)\in\left\{1,-1\right\}$ for $n\geq2$. The digit sequence $\left\{d_{n}(x)\right\}_{n\geq1}$ satisfying $d_{n+1}(x)\geq d_{n}(x)+2$ when $ε_{n+1}(x)=-ε_{n}(x)$ forms a non-decreasing sequence of even positive integers tending to infinity. On the one hand, we obtain the law of large numbers, the central limit theorem and the law of the iterated logarithm regarding $d_{n}(x)$ and $Δ_{n}(x)\coloneqq d_{n}(x)-d_{n-1}(x)\ (n\geq2)\ (Δ_{1}(x)\coloneqq d_{1}(x))$. On the other hand, we prove a Borel--Bernstein theorem on the zero-one law on the Lebesgue measure of the set
  $$\left\{x\in(0,1)\colon R_{n}(x)\geqφ(n)\ \textnormal{ for infinity many } n\right\},$$
  where $R_{n}(x)\coloneqq\frac{d_{n}(x)}{d_{n-1}(x)}\ (n\geq2)\ (R_{1}(x)\coloneqq d_{1}(x))$ and $φ$ is an arbitrary positive function defined on the set of positive integers.

</details>


### [128] [A unified proof of conjectures on the spaces of multiple $q$-zeta values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28584)
*Minoru Hirose,Takumi Maesaka,Taiki Watanabe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了关于多重q-zeta值生成空间的两个猜想：较小的空间Z_q^o和Z_{q,1}^o已经生成较大的空间Z_q和Z_{q,1}，且给出了整数系数的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证多重q-zeta值生成空间的两个猜想，即较小生成集是否足以生成整个空间。

Method: 先在有限层建立公式，利用生成级数给出有限q-模拟的递归描述，再取无穷极限过渡到一般情形。

Result: 证明了Z_q^o和Z_{q,1}^o分别生成Z_q和Z_{q,1}，且构造了整数系数的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 结果强于Q-向量空间相等，给出了生成元之间的显式整系数关系。

Abstract: We prove two conjectures on the spaces generated by multiple $q$-zeta values. More precisely, we show that the spaces $Z_q^{\mathrm{o}}$ and $Z_{q,1}^{\mathrm{o}}$ already generate the larger spaces $Z_q$ and $Z_{q,1}$, respectively. Our result is stronger than the equality of $\mathbb{Q}$-vector spaces: for every generator in the larger spaces, we construct an explicit expression with integer coefficients in terms of the smaller generating families. We first establish these formulas at the finite level, where suitable finite $q$-analogues admit recursive descriptions through generating series, and then pass to the infinite limit.

</details>


### [129] [On the analytic continuation of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28610)
*Paolo Valtancoli*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过详细分析黎曼ζ函数的解析延拓，推导出多个可能对物理和数学应用有用的新恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼ζ函数在数论和物理中具有重要地位，其解析延拓性质的研究有助于揭示更深层的数学结构，并可能为物理学中的正则化等问题提供新工具。

Method: 对黎曼ζ函数进行详细的解析延拓分析，通过严格推导生成新的恒等式。

Result: 生成了多个新的数学恒等式，这些恒等式在物理和数学领域具有潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: 所得到的新恒等式可为物理学和数学中的相关问题提供新的分析工具和视角。

Abstract: Analyzing in detail the analytic continuation of the Riemann zeta function we are able to generate several new identities which may be useful for application in physics and mathematics.

</details>


### [130] [Equidistribution of CM points and RM curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28614)
*Erick Ross,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了CM点和RM曲线沿固定测地线和固定点附近的分布问题，证明了它们沿每条有理测地线和每个CM点附近都是均匀分布的，并通过解决二元二次型的聚合Linnik问题来证明这些结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Duke关于CM点和RM曲线在上半平面均匀分布的经典结果推广到沿固定测地线和固定点附近的更精细的分布场景。

Method: 通过解决任意二元二次型的聚合Linnik问题来证明CM点和RM曲线沿有理测地线和CM点附近的均匀分布。

Result: CM点和RM曲线沿固定有理测地线以及围绕固定CM点都是均匀分布的。

Conclusion: 将Duke的均匀分布结果推广到了更精细的几何位置（测地线和点附近），并通过聚合Linnik问题这一统一方法实现了证明。

Abstract: In 1988, William Duke showed that CM points of fundamental discriminant $D$ are equidistributed in the complex upper half-plane $\mathcal H$ as $D \to -\infty$. He also showed a similar result for RM curves (a positive discriminant analog of CM points). In this paper, we investigate analogous problems concerning the distribution of CM points and RM curves along fixed geodesics in $\mathcal H$, and around fixed points in $\mathcal H$. Specifically, we show that CM points and RM curves are equidistributed along every fixed rational geodesic in $\mathcal H$, and around every fixed CM point in $\mathcal H$. To prove these results, we solve the aggregate Linnik problem for arbitrary binary quadratic forms.

</details>


### [131] [The sum-product conjecture is false for real numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28781)
*Thomas F Bloom,Will Sawin,Carl Schildkraut,Dmitrii Zhelezov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造反例，否定了实数域上的sum-product猜想和many sums and products猜想，并推广到p-adic数、有限域和正特征函数域中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典sum-product猜想认为对于实数集A，加法集A+A和乘法集AA不能同时很小，即max(|A+A|, |AA|) ≥ |A|^{2-o(1)}。本文旨在构造反例推翻这一猜想。

Method: 利用代数整数在数域中的结构性质构造集合A，其中数域的度数为~log|A|，使得加法和乘法结构同时受限，从而得到反例。

Result: (1) 构造了|A|任意大的实数集，使得max(|A+A|,|AA|) ≤ |A|^{2-c}，其中c>0为绝对常数。(2) 对任意k≥3，构造了max(|kA|,|A^{(k)}|) ≤ |A|^{C log k / log log k}的反例。

Conclusion: sum-product猜想在实数域上被否定，且在p-adic数、有限域和正特征函数域中也有类似构造；同时给出了线性方程解数和单位方程解数的新下界。

Abstract: We disprove the sum-product conjecture for real numbers by constructing arbitrarily large $A\subset \mathbb{R}$ (whose elements are algebraic integers in a number field of degree $\asymp \log\lvert A\rvert$) such that \[\max(\lvert A+A\rvert ,\lvert AA\rvert)\leq \lvert A\rvert^{2-c}\] where $c>0$ is an absolute constant.
  We also disprove the many sums and products conjecture by constructing, for any $k\geq 3$, arbitrarily large $A\subset \mathbb{R}$ such that \[\max(\lvert kA\rvert,\lvert A^{(k)}\rvert)\leq \lvert A\rvert^{C\frac{\log k}{\log\log k}}\] for some constant $C>0$. We obtain similar constructions for $p$-adics, finite fields, and function fields in positive characteristic, and also obtain new lower bounds for the number of solutions to linear equations in a multiplicative group and the number of solutions to the unit equation in sufficiently many variables.

</details>
